# Mathematical positioning of FEP Formal

Generated by `fep_lean.methods` from validated owner bytes and authored positioning policy.

Offline canonical source analysis and authored mathematical context. Declaration resolution is source-text validation, never native compilation. No Hermes, OpenGauss, upstream execution, live service, H3/Q7 gate, or scientific promotion occurs. Family context, numerical probes and distances are non-proof information.

Package version: `2.1.0`.

Source origin: `checkout`. Source hashes describe the bytes read for this analysis.

The projection contains 168 topics, 22 families, 14 overlapping domains, and 156 authored relations.

## Family coordinates

The classifications summarize reviewed invariants and carrier assumptions. A family domain is a union of its rows' concerns; each topic's exact scope follows below. Topic domain lists explicitly carry `inherited_family_context`, an editorial label rather than a semantic certification.

### core-free-energy

Carriers: native measures and ENNReal KL; measurable maps; scalar real quadratics; finite policy weights.

Scale: finite horizon; general measurable state; exact scalar continuous state.

Topology: Measurability alone supplies no topology; scalar contraction and Gaussian integration have their explicit real topology.

Embedding: Posterior equality is equality of measures; scalar quadratics are local models rather than embeddings of arbitrary generative models.

Statistics: Variational bounds constrain recognition laws; they do not identify the empirical data-generating mechanism.

Boundary: Objective descent and a fixed point do not identify an FEP agent or establish empirical adequacy.

### core-active-inference

Carriers: finite policy supports and matrices; ENNReal costs; native Markov posterior kernels.

Scale: finite horizon and support; general measurable posterior kernels.

Topology: Finite policy minimization needs no smooth manifold; kernel normalization needs the stated measurable structure.

Embedding: Reachable laws are a policy-map image; no injective policy coordinate or identifiable inverse is implied.

Statistics: Posterior reconstruction and information gain are carrier-specific; EFE sign depends on its real or truncating ENNReal convention.

Boundary: Finite argmin, softmax, and filtering do not establish general POMDP optimality or a full perception-action loop.

### core-bayesian-mechanics

Carriers: finite four-block partition; native measure kernels; Boolean sampling laws; real Gaussian law.

Scale: finite and measurable carriers; discrete-time two-state relaxation; scalar Gaussian continuous state.

Topology: A partition has set structure; sigma-algebra independence is distinct from geometric separation; Gaussian parameters have real topology.

Embedding: A 20-state assignment partitions states but does not learn a blanket; posterior kernels reconstruct a measure joint.

Statistics: Bernoulli sufficiency, conjugate closure, smoothing, and Brier properness retain normalization and evidence premises.

Boundary: A named block, a stationary law, and a physical Markov blanket require different additional arguments.

### core-information-geometry

Carriers: finite weighted tangent coordinates; Bernoulli unit interval; native ENNReal measure KL; scalar quadratic Bregman.

Scale: finite coordinates; one-dimensional statistical family; general measures.

Topology: Bernoulli Fisher is regular on the open interval; its coordinate distance extends to the closed interval without regular endpoint Fisher information.

Embedding: The Fisher-Rao coordinate pulls back a Euclidean metric; Hellinger and KL are distinct constructions with distinct domains.

Statistics: fep-004 supplies weights; fep-038 derives the Bernoulli weight and needs interior probabilities.

Boundary: Positive semidefiniteness is weaker than a nondegenerate Riemannian metric and provides no curvature theorem.

### core-thermodynamics

Carriers: scalar Helmholtz differential; finite Gibbs laws; two-state chains; finite edge fluxes; logical bit reset.

Scale: finite states; scalar temperature response; discrete-time relaxation.

Topology: Entropy differentiation uses positive real parameters; graph current cancellation is algebraic rather than continuum divergence theory.

Embedding: Thermodynamic free energy and variational free energy need an explicit energy map, units, and scaling to be identified.

Statistics: Maximum entropy and Gibbs normalization are distributional results; correlation-response is exact for the maintained two-state carrier.

Boundary: Landauer bounds retain total-entropy, temperature, heat, and work assumptions; compiled inequalities do not certify a physical erasure device.

### measure-bayesian-inversion

Carriers: native finite measures; Markov kernels; Radon-Nikodym derivatives; standard-Borel conditional kernels.

Scale: general measurable carriers under declared regularity; positive finite evidence atoms.

Topology: Standard-Borel regularity supports conditional-kernel constructions; it does not supply a manifold metric or arbitrary conditioning at null events.

Embedding: Bayesian inversion reconstructs joint measures, with almost-everywhere equivalence distinct from pointwise uniqueness.

Statistics: Posterior tilting and expectation towers describe exact model laws, not consistency or model identification from data.

Boundary: Absolute continuity, finite measures, evidence positivity, and almost-everywhere qualifications cannot be removed.

### variational-duality-and-information-bounds

Carriers: finite full-support laws; certified Gibbs optimizers; IID fixed-sample products; finite channels; rate-distortion feasible sets.

Scale: finite hypotheses and alphabets; fixed sample size.

Topology: The fixed-source finite-channel mass feasible set is compact and closed, continuous mutual information attains its constrained minimum even with zero atoms; this does not establish arbitrary functional-analytic duality or infinite-dimensional existence.

Embedding: Gibbs and KL certificates represent exact objective relationships; coordinate factorization is not latent identifiability.

Statistics: IID and positive importance weights are explicit; data-processing is channel-specific and rate-distortion conclusions follow the certified feasible carrier.

Boundary: Weak duality is not strong duality; a supplied optimizer certificate is not a general algorithm for finding it.

### control-and-planning-as-inference

Carriers: finite controlled kernels; finite reachable-belief indices; finite action scores; observation-dependent policies.

Scale: finite horizon; finite state and action sets.

Topology: Beliefs are finite laws with explicit normalization; the maintained reachable index is not the complete continuous belief simplex.

Embedding: Reachable-belief interpretation preserves a specified policy value; it need not enumerate every possible belief.

Statistics: Positive evidence enables Bayesian belief updates; soft posterior normalization alone does not identify a causal policy.

Boundary: The feedback advantage witness is concrete; continuous-state and infinite-horizon POMDP optimality are separate problems.

### temporal-and-hierarchical-inference

Carriers: finite hidden-state laws; backward messages; smoothing marginals; finite hierarchical kernels; finite model mixtures.

Scale: one-step recursion; finite levels and finite model classes.

Topology: Normalizers and finite products give algebraic factorization; no limiting smoother or infinite hierarchy is established.

Embedding: Forward-backward and hierarchical factorization preserve the authored joint law; latent labels can remain unidentifiable.

Statistics: Smoothing excludes zero normalizers; normalized variational updates do not establish optimization convergence.

Boundary: Finite model averaging is distinct from posterior model selection and asymptotic concentration.

### causal-blankets-and-interventions

Carriers: finite blanket-factorized laws; shared conditional kernels; deterministic intervention kernels; ordered four-node model.

Scale: finite states; fixed parent ordering.

Topology: Conditional independence concerns a probability law and sigma-algebras, rather than adjacency or geometric proximity.

Embedding: A do-assignment is an authored mechanism; recovering a causal graph from observational data is another inverse problem.

Statistics: Positive blanket or mediator evidence bounds the stated equivalences; conditional and marginal independence differ.

Boundary: No general d-separation, faithfulness, confounding control, or arbitrary-DAG identification follows from this fixed ordering.

### predictive-coding-and-generalized-coordinates

Carriers: precision-weighted scalar quadratics; finite hierarchy; finite jets and discrete shifts.

Scale: finite jet order; exact scalar real state; discrete iterations.

Topology: Real derivatives are exact for the scalar quadratic; finite jets do not establish an analytic infinite jet or continuous-time process.

Embedding: Jet shifts and correction equations encode declared coordinates; they are not learned neural representations.

Statistics: Positive precision gives separation; zero precision destroys it, and precision weighting alone supplies no noise-model calibration.

Boundary: Under nonnegative precision, the closed step-size interval [0,2] gives nonincreasing exact-quadratic energy; the open interval (0,2) gives scalar convergence, with strict decrease additionally requiring positive precision and nonzero error. This does not establish nonlinear predictive-coding stability.

### path-space-stochastic-thermodynamics

Carriers: normalized finite path laws; reverse-aligned path laws; full-support ratios; finite exponential-work protocols.

Scale: finite paths and protocols; one-step finite Markov contraction.

Topology: Finite path support is not a continuous path-space measure or stochastic differential equation.

Embedding: Reversal is an explicit map on the same path carrier; reference alignment and support are part of the information comparison.

Statistics: Path KL is oriented; log-ratio expectations and fluctuation equalities retain full-support and normalization premises.

Boundary: Zero reverse rates expose totalized-log boundaries; arbitrary physical work protocols and continuum fluctuation laws are not inferred.

### information-geometry-and-geometric-optimization

Carriers: categorical simplex tangents; finite Jacobians and Fisher matrices; supplied potential-gradient data; affine projection certificates.

Scale: finite-dimensional chart algebra; scalar estimation.

Topology: Full support and invertible charts bound regular Fisher geometry; a duplicated-score model records a nonzero null direction.

Embedding: Jacobian pullback composes exactly; rank-deficient reparameterization can lose tangent information.

Statistics: Cramer-Rao needs unbiasedness, score regularity, and positive information; it is not universal estimator performance.

Boundary: Three-point identities and a certified affine projection do not establish a manifold connection, curvature, or global mirror-descent convergence.

### collective-and-multiagent-active-inference

Carriers: independent product-agent laws; unit-weight product-of-experts pool; specified two-agent stochastic update.

Scale: two-agent products and concrete iterative consensus.

Topology: Product spaces and pointwise convergence describe the maintained update; no emergent collective manifold is constructed.

Embedding: Agent product factorization is an explicit independence choice; consensus pools laws without identifying shared latent semantics.

Statistics: Product-of-experts needs a positive normalizer; additivity relies on product laws and additive costs.

Boundary: Coupled-agent dependence, arbitrary networks, heterogeneous updates, and social-scientific validation require new models.

### learning-concentration-and-model-evidence

Carriers: finite independent sub-Gaussian samples; finite alphabets and hypotheses; certified log-MGF budget; two-hypothesis likelihood gap.

Scale: finite samples and hypothesis classes; explicit exponential bounds.

Topology: Concentration is a probability bound; it supplies no geometry of a learned representation.

Embedding: Posterior odds and evidence compare declared hypotheses; a likelihood gap is a supplied premise rather than learned identification.

Statistics: Population and empirical loss remain distinct; PAC-Bayes uses an explicit Gibbs and moment-generating-function certificate.

Boundary: These results do not establish dependent-data validity, universal generalization, or a true model in the hypothesis class.

### finite-sample-risk-and-calibration

Carriers: add-one Bernoulli forecast; normalized finite sampling law; Brier excess risk; deviation events.

Scale: positive finite sample size; exact pseudo-count transfer.

Topology: Unit-interval smoothing moves forecasts to the interior; it does not alter the support assumptions of another theorem.

Embedding: A smoothed forecast is an explicit statistic of successes and sample size; the map can discard sample-order information.

Statistics: Pointwise error identities lift to finite expected risk; supplied empirical-event bounds transfer without proving the original concentration premise.

Boundary: Proper Brier scoring and risk transfer do not imply calibration on arbitrary held-out populations or asymptotic posterior consistency.

### closed-loop-policy-trees-and-efe

Carriers: finite-depth observation-contingent trees; finite action minima; embedded open-loop plans; Boolean feedback witness.

Scale: finite depth; finite action and observation carriers.

Topology: Recursion is finite combinatorial structure; no compactness or limiting infinite tree is used.

Embedding: An open-loop plan embeds by repeating one continuation across observations, preserving value on the declared carrier.

Statistics: Treewise EFE decomposition retains one-step full-support conditions at branches; feedback uses informative observations in a concrete model.

Boundary: Finite-tree dominance does not extend automatically to infinite horizons, partially observed continuous dynamics, or empirical agent behavior.

### finite-to-native-blanket-transfer

Carriers: finite weighted Dirac measures; embedded finite kernels; blanket-factorized static and transition laws.

Scale: finite atom laws inside native measure theory.

Topology: Measurable coarsening preserves the native independence predicate; finite embedding is not a continuum approximation limit.

Embedding: Weighted Dirac embedding is injective and transfers expectations and prediction; native syntax does not increase source support.

Statistics: Rectangle factorization supplies native conditional independence under the authored blanket law.

Boundary: Finite-to-native transfer does not derive a physical blanket, arbitrary continuous conditional laws, or identifiability from observations.

### finite-exponential-family-dual-geometry

Carriers: finite full-support scalar exponential family; real natural and mean parameters; log-partition potential.

Scale: finite sample alphabet; scalar parameter; closed parameter interval.

Topology: Positive statistic variance gives a regular local information coordinate and injectivity on the stated interval; constant statistics have zero curvature.

Embedding: Natural-to-mean coordinates are injective only under the variance premise; KL equals an oriented log-partition Bregman divergence on this supported family.

Statistics: The score is centered and Fisher equals variance of the supplied sufficient statistic.

Boundary: This finite scalar family is not an arbitrary exponential family, multivariate dual manifold, or generic latent embedding.

### two-state-continuous-time-thermodynamics

Carriers: Boolean state law; positive forward and backward rates; explicit real-time transition matrix.

Scale: two states; nonnegative continuous time.

Topology: Time is continuous and transition entries differentiable; the state carrier is still finite.

Embedding: The stationary law is explicit and can be nonuniform; the squared-deviation Lyapunov function is a declared scalar diagnostic.

Statistics: Normalization, detailed balance, and exact relaxation are distributional laws under positive rates.

Boundary: Quadratic Lyapunov decay is not physical free-energy identification or a general CTMC existence theorem.

### standalone-efe-formalizations

Carriers: finite Boltzmann policy law; closed-loop finite kernel; masked model evidence; two-state rate law; stationary law-weighted split.

Scale: finite policies and states; fixed precision; two-state continuous time.

Topology: Exact finite decompositions and rate limits remain attached to their specified carrier; they do not solve a continuum Fokker-Planck equation.

Embedding: Law-weighted reversible and circulation parts are unique under their exact weighted symmetry constraints; signed circulation parts need not be Markov rates.

Statistics: Policy posterior and evidence reduction retain model and support premises; a loop invariant alone does not identify a universal perception-action mechanism.

Boundary: A paired conjunction is distinct from a derivational theorem edge; entropy-production labels need the stated physical interpretation premises.

### geometric-mechanics-notation

Carriers: finite real matrices; skew and symmetric forms; discrete Hessian symmetrizer; graph node divergence.

Scale: finite-dimensional matrix and finite-node carriers.

Topology: Symmetrizing a finite matrix constructs a discrete Hessian; it does not prove smooth mixed-partial equality or continuum differential topology.

Embedding: Frobenius projection onto symmetric matrices has a unique minimizer; plain skew symmetry and law-weighted skewness are different constraints.

Statistics: Least-squares here is deterministic matrix geometry; no data noise distribution or estimator risk is supplied.

Boundary: Skew cancellation leaves a divergence remainder unless an extra premise holds; the explicit two-node witness refutes an unconditional solenoidal drop.

## Continuous native scope beyond catalog families

These selected authored scope notes concern manifested source modules. The complete inventory is in the JSON projection. Listing a declaration here does not validate a native receipt or close its scientific acceptance gates.

## Common FEP / OpenAI mathematical coordinates

Positive authored feature assignments. Absence means not assigned, not logical negation. FEP rows are family unions; upstream rows are individually named result units. Hypothesis axes include stated premises and regimes, not a common proposition.

Each authored coordinate has unit weight. Domain/carrier/premise groups with more coordinates contribute more potential mismatches; no hidden group normalization.

Feature proximity creates no relation edge, equivalence witness, imported theorem, native evidence, or scientific promotion.

| Coordinate | Kind | Definition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| domain:algebra | domain | finite sums, matrices, identities, and algebraic constructions; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:analysis | domain | real differentiation, integration, inequalities, and limits; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:topology | domain | compactness, closed feasible carriers, continuity, and weak convergence; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:probability | domain | normalized laws, kernels, independence, and stochastic evolution; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:measure-theory | domain | sigma-algebras, native measures, disintegration, and integration; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:information-theory | domain | entropy, relative entropy, information, and coding bounds; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:statistics | domain | estimation, scoring rules, concentration, and model evidence; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:optimization | domain | variational bounds, minimization, descent, and projection; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:control | domain | finite planning, feedback, decision rules, and policy recursion; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:dynamical-systems | domain | iterates, semigroups, stationarity, and convergence; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:differential-geometry | domain | Fisher pairings, coordinate pullbacks, and dual coordinates; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:graph-theory | domain | finite causal ordering, node divergence, and edge currents; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:causal-inference | domain | authored intervention mechanisms and conditional factorization; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| domain:thermodynamics | domain | entropy-production and free-energy identities on explicit carriers; positive editorial mathematical context. |
| carrier:finite-discrete | carrier | A selected law, chain, message, policy or map has a finite discrete carrier; this is not inferred from finite storage of a continuous object. |
| carrier:finite-dimensional-real | carrier | The selected statement uses actual real scalar/vector/matrix data. This coordinate alone does not imply a smooth manifold or identifiable parameters. |
| carrier:general-measurable | carrier | Native sigma-algebras, measures, kernels or Borel laws are explicit objects in the selected contract, possibly together with finite carriers. |
| carrier:euclidean-space | carrier | An ambient Euclidean geometric carrier is explicitly used in the selected result, beyond real-valued scalar arithmetic. |
| carrier:function-space | carrier | The selected result quantifies over functions, trial laws, controls, or an infinite-dimensional normed carrier; no common Banach structure is implied. |
| carrier:metric-space | carrier | A declared metric/norm topology is part of the selected contract, with its own distance; this is not the MDS feature-space metric. |
| carrier:graph | carrier | A finite graph, node/edge carrier or graph-realization state is explicit; graphical conditional independence alone is insufficient. |
| carrier:path-space | carrier | Path laws, time-indexed protocols or progressively measurable controls are explicit; this does not identify a common physical path carrier. |
| hypothesis:normalization | hypothesis | A normalized law/kernel or a normalization condition/conclusion is explicit in the selected statement. It is not asserted to be a premise of every family theorem. |
| hypothesis:full-support | hypothesis | Strictly positive mass/density on the stated whole law carrier is explicit. Local selected support and positive evidence at one event do not suffice. |
| hypothesis:regularity | hypothesis | An explicit measurable, continuous, differentiable, completeness or analytic parameter regularity condition occurs; those distinct notions are not interchangeable. |
| hypothesis:convexity | hypothesis | Convex body, log-concavity or convex-potential curvature is explicit in the selected contract; no shared uniform convexity hypothesis is inferred. |
| hypothesis:compactness | hypothesis | A compact carrier/feasible set or its compactness-based attainment argument is explicit. Finite-dimensionality alone does not suffice. |
| hypothesis:independence | hypothesis | Independence, product-law structure or reversible conditional-factorization inputs occur explicitly. This does not identify empirical or causal independence. |
| hypothesis:finite-horizon | hypothesis | A fixed finite time/horizon or finite-time bound is part of the selected contract. It does not assert an infinite-horizon or stopping-time result. |
| hypothesis:asymptotic-limit | hypothesis | A convergence or dimension/system-size limit is a stated target or regime. Despite this coordinate group name it is not a logical hypothesis by itself. |

### openai-math-093-dimension-free-lsi: A dimension-free logarithmic Sobolev inequality for subgaussian log-concave measures

Pinned statement source: [preprints/A-dimension-free-logarithmic-Sobolev-inequality-for-subgaussian-log-concave-measures-September-23-2026/build/sections/01-introduction.tex](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/preprints/A-dimension-free-logarithmic-Sobolev-inequality-for-subgaussian-log-concave-measures-September-23-2026/build/sections/01-introduction.tex); SHA-256 `6b737533c3be85eed78ccee2cc3503eb812ac746cac2a55147cb5d4f0b4324f4`. Status: `reviewed_source_statement_uncompiled`.

Dimension-free LSI: for every centered log-concave probability density on R^n, n>=1, whose every unit linear marginal satisfies E exp(<X,theta>^2/a^2)<=2, an absolute C gives Ent_mu(f^2)<=C a^2 integral|Df|^2 dmu for all compactly supported smooth f.

Carriers: Euclidean R^n; Lebesgue-density probability law; Smooth compactly supported real test functions

Hypotheses/context: n>=1; a>0; Centered log-concave density; Uniform linear subgaussian exponential-moment bound

Scope: Paper thm:main; cor:intro-transport additionally asserts W2(nu,mu)^2<=C a^2 H(nu|mu) for nu in P2, with extended relative entropy. No linked Lean scope record was found. Density smoothness and strictly positive Hessian curvature are not premises. The upstream catalogue review is unchecked; no solution compilation or Comparator execution was performed here.

Assignment rationale: Relates native entropy and Dirichlet energy to transport distance; an FEP descent statement needs a matching diffusion generator, density ratio, support convention and functional-inequality constant before borrowing this route.

### openai-math-140-gaussian-replica-block: Posterior replicas and conditional information in Gaussian regression

Pinned statement source: [preprints/Posterior-replicas-and-conditional-information-in-Gaussian-regression-September-27-2026/build/sections/00-introduction.tex](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/preprints/Posterior-replicas-and-conditional-information-in-Gaussian-regression-September-27-2026/build/sections/00-introduction.tex); SHA-256 `b892d5e4eb9498a44f3838c5dff684c91b9c5f509d26c4ac70959bbbe5c57d5d`. Gaussian replica block bound: for large d, k=m=2 floor(d/16), t=m+1 and ell=4k, a spherical prior p=f sigma_d with 0<=f<=L and a finite message W from k exact Gaussian labels satisfies I(S;W|B,BS)<=H(W)/t+C d+C log(2+log L), for an independent ell-row Gaussian matrix B.

Carriers: Unit sphere in R^d; Bounded Borel prior density relative to uniform surface law; Exact linear observations and finite message kernels

Hypotheses/context: Large d and probability density 0<=f<=L<infinity; Independent standard Gaussian A and B; W drawn from a measurable data kernel, conditionally independent of S given (A,AS)

The upstream catalogue review is unchecked; no solution compilation or Comparator execution was performed here.

Assignment rationale: Compare posterior disintegration and information lost through observation fibers; exact-label singular geometry, bounded prior density and message memory differ from noisy Gaussian filtering or unrestricted recognition maps.

Pinned statement source: [preprints/The-Mezard-Parisi-formula-for-diluted-spin-glasses-September-23-2026/build/sections/model.tex](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/preprints/The-Mezard-Parisi-formula-for-diluted-spin-glasses-September-23-2026/build/sections/model.tex); SHA-256 `8794914e5e7dbe802de6ee006c5e398bdae2790be27898054e5ac4c20ec18487`. Diluted pressure limit: for fixed even arity p>=2 and alpha>0 in the stated independent Poisson-diluted Ising model, first-moment integrability and Panchenko-Talagrand factorization/positivity imply lim_N N^-1 E log Z_N = inf_{r>=0} Phi_r over finite hierarchy depths and trial laws.

Carriers: Finite Ising spin configurations; Poisson interaction disorder; Nested Borel probability laws with weak-convergence topology

Hypotheses/context: Mutually independent disorder and with-replacement uniform site indices; E max_s|theta(s)|+E|h|<infinity; exp(theta)=a(1+b product f_l), a>0, strict product bound and E[(-b)^n]>=0

Scope: Paper main:theorem/main:formula and lean/docs/221.md. The formula takes an infimum, with no claimed attaining trial law or constructed infinite-depth Gibbs state. Interaction/disorder laws stay fixed as N varies. The upstream catalogue review is unchecked; no solution compilation or Comparator execution was performed here.

Assignment rationale: A precise bridge from finite Gibbs normalization to asymptotic variational pressure; product-agent additivity alone supplies neither random disorder, thermodynamic scaling nor cavity hierarchy assumptions.

Carriers: Growing Euclidean spheres; Gaussian pattern disorder; Overlap quantile functions; Brownian progressively measurable variational controls

Hypotheses/context: Fixed alpha,beta>0 and phi bounded continuous; Independent standard Gaussian patterns; Uniform sphere probability normalization; Nondecreasing measurable trials; infinite entropy assigned positive infinity

### openai-math-360-uniform-bi-holder-transport: Uniform Bi-Hölder Transport from Weak MTW

Pinned statement source: [preprints/Uniform-Bi-Holder-Transport-from-Weak-MTW-September-25-2026/build/sections/introduction.tex](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/preprints/Uniform-Bi-Holder-Transport-from-Weak-MTW-September-25-2026/build/sections/introduction.tex); SHA-256 `08e697324bf067dcc76fc938bb123919080b245e591ab40142c9bc9a3b6681b6`. Uniform bi-Holder transport: on one fixed compact connected smooth boundaryless Riemannian manifold of dimension>=2 satisfying weak MTW, normalized measurable densities between fixed positive lower and finite upper bounds have a.e.-unique quadratic-cost optimal maps with homeomorphic representatives whose maps and inverses share one Holder exponent and constant.

Carriers: Compact smooth Riemannian manifold; Volume-density probability laws; Homeomorphisms and squared-distance transport maps

Hypotheses/context: Dimension>=2 and weak MTW at smooth cost pairs; Fixed0<lambda<=Lambda with a nonempty density class; lambda<=rho_i<=Lambda a.e. and integrals1

Scope: Paper thm:main and lean/docs/360.md. Constants depend on the fixed metric and density bounds, not varying metrics. No explicit exponent, strict MTW, nonfocality or cut avoidance is asserted. Companion convex-injectivity support is a separate result unit. The upstream catalogue review is unchecked; no solution compilation or Comparator execution was performed here.

Assignment rationale: Transport topology needs an actual cost and density class. Fisher/KL identities or a parameter chart do not establish MTW curvature, global homeomorphism or inverse-map regularity.

### openai-math-374-one-third-map-stability: Sharp One-Third Stability of Brenier Maps

Pinned statement source: [preprints/Sharp-One-Third-Stability-of-Brenier-Maps-September-25-2026/build/source/sections/introduction.tex](https://github.com/openai/math/blob/adc7f1241b42e322a6451854ab7e4b4c146bf78a/preprints/Sharp-One-Third-Stability-of-Brenier-Maps-September-25-2026/build/source/sections/introduction.tex); SHA-256 `73d98e5f63dc7d89b91ccb5f47737458a84a463288651ca83ae02f930c9c8b60`. Status: `reviewed_source_statement_uncompiled`.

One-third Brenier stability: for uniform source rho on a fixed compact convex body K with interior in R^d, d>=2, and arbitrary Borel targets mu,nu in a fixed nonempty compact Y, quadratic optimal maps satisfy ||T_mu-T_nu||_{L2(rho)}<=C(K,Y) W2(mu,nu)^(1/3).

Carriers: Euclidean compact convex source body; Arbitrary compactly supported probability targets; Convex-gradient optimal maps in L2(rho)

Hypotheses/context: d>=2; source body has nonempty interior; Source law uniform on fixed K; Targets supported on fixed nonempty compact Y

Scope: Paper main:stability and lean/docs/374.md include sharpness: fixed-cube three-atom targets refute every uniform exponent>1/3. Atomic/singular targets and vanishing masses are allowed; no target density lower bound or site separation is required. The upstream catalogue review is unchecked; no solution compilation or Comparator execution was performed here.

Assignment rationale: Perturbing a target law is not perturbing a recognition chart: compare W2 on laws against L2 map error and retain the uniform-source/compact-target hypotheses; a square-root map bound can fail.

Entropy-rate dimension: for finite indexed similarities phi_i(x)=r_i x+t_i on R with0<|r_i|<1 and strictly positive probability weights, the self-similar law has lower Hausdorff dimension min(1,h_RW/chi), where h_RW is entropy of exact random composed affine maps and chi=-sum p_i log|r_i|.

### `gaussian_information_geometry.lean`

Native scalar Gaussian location family at one fixed strictly positive variance.

Derived density, normalization, absolute continuity, scores, and Fisher coordinates; zero variance and general manifolds remain outside this owner.

### `smooth_information_geometry.lean`

Scalar Gaussian natural and mean charts with an explicit duplicated-coordinate pullback.

Metric pairings and affine coordinate paths, including a rank-deficient countermodel; no connection, curvature, or tangent-bundle hierarchy.

### `scalar_gaussian_semigroup.lean`

Scalar real Gaussian transition kernel with explicit variance evolution.

Native finite-time semigroup and weak-convergence laws; process existence and empirical interpretation are separate claims.

### `linear_gaussian_semigroup.lean`

Finite-dimensional Euclidean Gaussian kernels with declared linear drift and covariance.

Finite-time native Gaussian composition, stationarity, and weak convergence on the authored symmetric-precision linear carrier, not arbitrary nonlinear diffusion.

### `fin4_gaussian_semigroup.lean`

Exact four-axis Euclidean Gaussian carrier with named external, sensory, active, and internal coordinates.

Native coordinate and Gaussian semigroup results do not identify a causal graph or a physical agent.

### `gaussian_precision_conditioning.lean`

Native Gaussian precision blocks and their conditional-law construction.

Conditioning belongs to the exact positive-definite Gaussian carrier; a block label does not supply causal or observational identifiability.

### `h3_reference_model.lean`

Frozen four-axis native Gaussian reference with positive affine calibration of tagged readings.

Constructs finite-time laws and intrinsic recognition algebra; no stochastic-process existence, empirical validity, causal graph, or physical heat follows.

### fep-001: Variational Free Energy Bound

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep001.FEP001.fep001_variationalUpperBound_eq_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP001`.

Invariant: Surprisal plus Mathlib's native measure KL upper-bounds surprisal; for finite measures and finite surprisal, equality holds exactly when the approximate and exact posterior laws coincide.

Assumptions: The inequality is unconditional in ENNReal. Gap separation requires finite measures, and bound equality additionally requires surprisal below infinity so additive cancellation is sound.

Non-vacuity: Matching finite posterior laws attain equality, while any unequal finite pair has a nonzero KL gap and hence a strict finite-surprisal remainder.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep001_variationalUpperBound_ge, fep001_variationalGap_eq_zero_iff, and fep001_variationalUpperBound_eq_iff.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep001_variationalUpperBound_eq_iff
    (approximation posterior : Measure α)
    [IsFiniteMeasure approximation] [IsFiniteMeasure posterior]
    {surprisal : ENNReal} (hsurprisal : surprisal ≠ ∞) :
    fep001_variationalUpperBound approximation posterior surprisal = surprisal ↔
      approximation = posterior
```

### fep-002: Variational Evidence Bound via KL Divergence

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep002.FEP002.fep002_vfe_ge_surprisal`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP002`.

Invariant: Variational free energy, defined as surprisal plus Mathlib KL divergence, upper-bounds surprisal and is exact at the posterior.

Assumptions: The bound is unconditional over measures in a measurable space; exactness uses Mathlib's SigmaFinite assumption for KL self-divergence. The codomain is ENNReal, so infinite divergences are represented without a finiteness side condition.

Non-vacuity: Any sigma-finite posterior supplies the equality witness q = posterior, while distinct measures can produce a nonzero or infinite KL remainder.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep002_vfe_ge_surprisal and fep002_vfe_exact_at_posterior, using Mathlib's InformationTheory.klDiv rather than an assumed scalar.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
    surprisal ≤ fep002_variationalFreeEnergy q posterior surprisal
```

### fep-003: Discounted Pragmatic Cost

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep003.FEP003.fep003_pragmaticCost_succ`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP003`.

Invariant: Finite-horizon pragmatic cost is the discounted ENNReal sum of stage costs; horizon extension exposes the exact terminal increment, costs are pointwise monotone, and horizon extension is monotone.

Assumptions: All costs and the discount live in ENNReal, so nonnegativity and infinity are represented directly. This is the pragmatic-cost input to EFE; epistemic value and its sign are owned by fep-021.

Non-vacuity: For positive discount and positive new stage cost, the terminal increment is nonzero; strict growth of the total additionally requires the previous accumulated cost to be finite. Discount zero at positive horizons exposes the zero-increment boundary.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep003_pragmaticCost_zero, fep003_pragmaticCost_succ, fep003_pragmaticCost_mono, and fep003_pragmaticCost_horizon_succ.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep003_pragmaticCost_succ
    (discount : ENNReal) (stageCost : ℕ → ENNReal) (horizon : ℕ) :
    fep003_pragmaticCost discount stageCost (horizon + 1) =
      fep003_pragmaticCost discount stageCost horizon +
        discount ^ horizon * stageCost horizon
```

### fep-004: Finite Diagonal Fisher Metric

Family: `core-information-geometry`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep004.FEP004.fep004_fisherMetric_eq_zero_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_information_geometry.py`; canonical namespace `FEP004`.

Invariant: A finite diagonal Fisher metric is symmetric and positive semidefinite under nonnegative information weights, and becomes positive definite under strictly positive weights.

Assumptions: The information weights are explicit inputs rather than derived from a statistical family in this row. Positive definiteness requires every coordinate weight to be strictly positive; fep-038 computes the one-dimensional Bernoulli weight and the composed theorem identifies that specialization.

Non-vacuity: Any strictly positive nonconstant weight vector and nonzero tangent has positive norm, while the zero-characterization excludes every nonzero coordinate direction.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep004_fisherMetric_symm, fep004_fisherMetric_nonneg, fep004_fisherMetric_eq_zero_iff, and FEPComposed.fep004_bernoulliMetric_specialization.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.


### fep-005: Four-Block State Partition

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep005.FEP005.fep005_existsUnique_block`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP005`.

Invariant: A function-valued assignment partitions a 20-state finite space into four pairwise-disjoint blocks, and every state belongs to exactly one block.

Assumptions: Unconditional for any assignment Fin 20 → Fin 4. This establishes an exact finite partition only; it does not infer conditional independence or identify any block as a learned Markov blanket.

Non-vacuity: Constant and nonconstant assignments both instantiate the partition, while unique membership rules out overlap for every state.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep005_disjoint, fep005_mem_iff, and fep005_existsUnique_block, plus a concrete nonconstant assignment witness.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. ### fep-006: Measurable Discrete-Time Flow

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep006.FEP006.fep006_iterateFlow_add`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP006`.

Invariant: Iteration of one measurable state update defines a measurable discrete-time flow with identity at time zero, an exact additive semigroup law, and persistence of fixed points.

Assumptions: Measurability of each time slice requires the one-step map to be measurable. The algebraic semigroup and fixed-point laws require no topology, invariant measure, differentiability, or continuous-time interpolation.

Non-vacuity: Identity, constant, and nontrivial measurable maps instantiate the flow; the additive law constrains every pair of horizons rather than a single composition.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep006_iterateFlow_measurable, fep006_iterateFlow_zero, fep006_iterateFlow_add, and fep006_iterateFlow_fixed.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. ### fep-007: Normalized Finite Sum-Product Message

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep007.FEP007.fep007_normalizedMessage_sum_one`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP007`.

Invariant: Strictly positive local factors and incoming messages over a nonempty finite neighbor support define a nonnegative normalized sum-product message with support mass exactly one.

Assumptions: Normalization requires a nonempty selected support and strict positivity of every factor and incoming message on it. The message is zero outside that support. This is one exact local update, not loopy-belief-propagation convergence or global marginal correctness.

Non-vacuity: Singleton and multi-neighbor supports produce positive normalizers; unequal factor-message products yield a genuinely nonuniform normalized message.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep007_messageNormalizer_pos, fep007_normalizedMessage_nonneg, and fep007_normalizedMessage_sum_one.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep007_normalizedMessage_sum_one
    (ψ : Node → Node → ℝ) (incoming : Node → ℝ)
    (neighbors : Finset Node) (i : Node)
    (hne : neighbors.Nonempty)
    (hψ : ∀ j ∈ neighbors, 0 < ψ i j)
    (hincoming : ∀ j ∈ neighbors, 0 < incoming j) :
    ∑ j ∈ neighbors,
      fep007_normalizedMessage ψ incoming neighbors i j = 1
```

### fep-008: Finite Policy Objective Minimizer

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP008`.

### fep-009: Conditional Independence Laws

Primary theorem: `fep_fep009.FEP009.fep009_condIndep_symm`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP009`.

Invariant: Conditional independence of two σ-algebras given a third is symmetric under Mathlib's conditional-expectation-kernel definition.

Assumptions: Conditional on a standard Borel measurable space, a finite measure, a conditioning σ-algebra below the ambient one, and the stated conditional-independence relation. The theorem is a law of conditional independence, not a learned Markov-blanket factorization.

Non-vacuity: fep009_condIndep_bot_right proves that every σ-algebra is conditionally independent of the trivial σ-algebra, so the relation is inhabited.

### fep-011: Surprise and Self-Information

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep011.FEP011.fep011_surprise_eq_zero_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP011`.

Invariant: Negative-log self-information is nonnegative on positive probabilities at most one, additive on positive products, strictly antitone, and zero exactly at certainty.

Assumptions: Positivity excludes the logarithmic boundary, and the probability interpretation additionally uses p≤1. Additivity is scalar and corresponds to product probabilities; no independence relation is inferred by the theorem.

Non-vacuity: Every p in (0,1) has strictly positive surprise, p=1 is the unique zero, and two positive factors exercise the additive law.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep011_surprise_nonneg, fep011_surprise_additive, fep011_surprise_eq_zero_iff, and fep011_surprise_strictAnti.

### fep-012: Finite Policy Shannon Entropy Regularization

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep012.FEP012.fep012_entropyRegularizedCost_le_expectedCost`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP012`.

Invariant: Finite Shannon policy entropy is nonnegative on unit-interval weights, vanishes for point policies, and at nonnegative temperature lowers expected cost by exactly its entropy reward.

Assumptions: Entropy nonnegativity only requires each weight in [0,1]; normalization is supplied separately by a policy law such as fep-028 softmax. The theorem compares objectives and does not claim that softmax is the unique optimizer.

Non-vacuity: A point mass has zero entropy while a multi-policy interior law has positive terms; positive temperature yields a nonzero objective correction whenever entropy is positive.

### fep-013: Equilibrium Helmholtz Differential Identities

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep013.FEP013.fep013_helmholtz_derivative_eq_neg_entropy`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP013`.

Invariant: For differentiable U(T) and S(T), Helmholtz free energy has derivative U'−S−TS'; under the equilibrium first-law identity U'=TS', this reduces exactly to dF/dT=−S.

Assumptions: The derivative law is conditional on pointwise differentiability and the explicitly stated equilibrium identity. It is a thermodynamic calculus identity, not a derivation of the first law or an identification with variational free energy.

Non-vacuity: The fep040 Gaussian thermal entropy with U(T)=T/2 satisfies the premise at positive scale and temperature, yielding a nonconstant mathematical model with explicit dimensionless units.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep013_helmholtz_hasDerivAt, fep013_helmholtz_derivative_eq_neg_entropy, and FEPComposed.fep013_gaussianHelmholtz_derivative.

### fep-014: KL Divergence: Non-Negativity, Identity, and Chain Rule

Family: `core-information-geometry`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep014.FEP014.fep014_kl_chain_rule`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_information_geometry.py`; canonical namespace `FEP014`.

Invariant: Mathlib KL divergence is nonnegative, vanishes exactly on equal finite measures, and obeys the composition-product chain rule for Markov kernels.

Assumptions: Self-divergence uses SigmaFinite; zero-characterization and the chain rule use finite measures; the chain rule additionally requires Markov kernels. The pin separately exposes native measure-KL data processing under a Markov kernel as InformationTheory.klDiv_comp_right_le; fep-014 does not include that theorem in its maintained theorem surface.

Non-vacuity: Equal finite measures witness zero KL, unequal finite measures exercise the zero-characterization, and distinct Markov kernels give a nontrivial conditional term in the chain rule.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep014_kl_nonneg, fep014_kl_self, fep014_kl_eq_zero_iff, and fep014_kl_chain_rule against Mathlib.InformationTheory.KullbackLeibler.ChainRule.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep014_kl_chain_rule (μ ν : Measure α) (κ η : Kernel α β)
    [IsFiniteMeasure μ] [IsFiniteMeasure ν]
    [IsMarkovKernel κ] [IsMarkovKernel η] :
    InformationTheory.klDiv (μ ⊗ₘ κ) (ν ⊗ₘ η) =
      InformationTheory.klDiv μ ν +
        InformationTheory.klDiv (μ ⊗ₘ κ) (μ ⊗ₘ η)
```

### fep-015: Measurable Variational Integrand

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep015.FEP015.fep015_variationalIntegrand_measurable`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP015`.

Invariant: A pointwise variational integrand built from measurable energy, approximate log density, and generative log density is measurable and remains so under measurable reparameterization and baseline shifts.

Assumptions: All three real-valued ingredients and any reparameterization are assumed measurable. Measurability does not imply integrability, finite expectation, or existence of an optimizer, none of which is claimed.

Non-vacuity: Nonconstant measurable coordinate functions instantiate all three terms, and a nonidentity measurable reparameterization exercises the composition theorem.

### fep-016: Exact Scalar Quadratic Laplace Kernel

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep016.FEP016.fep016_laplaceKernel_integral`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP016`.

Invariant: A positive-precision scalar quadratic exponential is integrable and has the exact Gaussian normalizing integral sqrt(π/(precision/2)).

Assumptions: Precision is strictly positive for the scientific integrability result. This is the exact scalar quadratic case underlying Laplace approximation; no approximation-error or higher-dimensional determinant theorem is claimed.

Non-vacuity: At precision two the kernel is exp(−x²) with normalizer sqrt(π), and every positive precision gives a nonconstant integrable density kernel.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep016_precision_weighted, fep016_laplaceKernel_integrable, and fep016_laplaceKernel_integral against Mathlib's Gaussian integral.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. theorem fep016_laplaceKernel_integral
```lean
    {precision : ℝ} (hprecision : 0 < precision) :
    (∫ x : ℝ, fep016_laplaceKernel precision x) =
      Real.sqrt (Real.pi / (precision / 2))
```

### fep-017: Posterior Kernel Reconstruction

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep017.FEP017.fep017_posterior_joint_reconstruction`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP017`.

Invariant: Mathlib's posterior is a Markov kernel whose composition with the predictive law reconstructs the swapped prior-likelihood joint law; for a Markov likelihood it also recovers the prior.

Assumptions: Conditional on standard-Borel and nonempty latent structure plus finite prior and likelihood kernel. The Radon--Nikodym Bayes formula additionally uses countability of the latent space and standard-Borel observation structure. This is a regular posterior-kernel theorem, not a claim about learned or approximate posteriors. The Bayes-density formula additionally assumes countable latent and nonempty standard-Borel observation structure and holds only almost everywhere under the predictive law. Every selected posterior fibre is normalized, including version choices at null observations.

Non-vacuity: Every posterior fibre has mass one, and the identity Markov kernel supplies a concrete prior-recovery witness; non-deterministic finite kernels exercise a nontrivial reconstructed joint law.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep017_posterior_mass_one, fep017_posterior_joint_reconstruction, fep017_posterior_recovers_prior, and fep017_posterior_bayes_density.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
theorem fep017_posterior_joint_reconstruction
    (κ : Kernel Ω 𝓧) (μ : Measure Ω)
    [StandardBorelSpace Ω] [Nonempty Ω]
    [IsFiniteMeasure μ] [IsFiniteKernel κ] :
    (κ ∘ₘ μ) ⊗ₘ fep017_posterior κ μ = (μ ⊗ₘ κ).map Prod.swap
```

### fep-018: Bernoulli Fisher–Rao Coordinate Distance

Family: `core-information-geometry`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep018.FEP018.fep018_fisherRaoDistance_eq_zero_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_information_geometry.py`; canonical namespace `FEP018`.

Invariant: The Bernoulli Fisher--Rao coordinate distance is nonnegative, symmetric, separating on the probability interval, and satisfies the triangle inequality.

Assumptions: Separation is restricted to Bernoulli parameters in [0,1], where square root and arcsine are injective. This is the closed-form one-dimensional Fisher--Rao distance, not a general geodesic-existence theorem for arbitrary statistical manifolds.

Non-vacuity: The endpoint parameters p = 0 and q = 1 have distinct coordinates, while every equal pair has zero distance.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep018_fisherRaoDistance_nonneg, fep018_fisherRaoDistance_symm, fep018_fisherRaoDistance_triangle, and fep018_fisherRaoDistance_eq_zero_iff.

### fep-019: Prior-Predictive Kernel Composition

### fep-020: Two-State Markov Relaxation

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep020.FEP020.fep020_tendsto_uniform`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP020`.

Invariant: A normalized symmetric two-state transition has the uniform stationary law; its exact n-step deviation is (1-2α)^n times the initial deviation and converges to zero for 0 < α < 1.

Assumptions: Transition nonnegativity uses α in [0,1], while convergence uses the strict interior so |1-2α| < 1. This is an exact finite-state Markov relaxation model, not a discretized Langevin diffusion.

Non-vacuity: For α = 1/4 and any nonstationary initial mass, deviations contract geometrically by 1/2 at every step; at α = 1 the chain instead alternates and the strict-interior convergence premise excludes it.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep020_transition_nonneg, fep020_transition_sum_one, fep020_uniform_stationary, fep020_iterate_deviation, and fep020_tendsto_uniform.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
    Filter.Tendsto (fun n => ((fep020_evolve α)^[n]) p)
      Filter.atTop (nhds (1 / 2 : ℝ))
```

### fep-021: EFE Epistemic–Pragmatic Balance

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep021.FEP021.fep021_efe_epistemic_balance`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP021`.

Invariant: Expected free energy is pragmatic expected cost minus epistemic information value in ENNReal; under epistemic value at most cost, adding information back reconstructs cost, while EFE is monotone in cost and antitone in information.

Assumptions: The truncated subtraction convention makes EFE nonnegative and requires an explicit epistemic≤pragmatic premise for exact reconstruction. It does not assert the alternative risk-plus-ambiguity identity without a generative-model derivation.

Non-vacuity: Strictly positive pragmatic cost with zero information yields positive EFE, equal cost and information yields zero, and intermediate information lowers EFE without changing sign.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep021_efe_epistemic_balance, fep021_efe_mono_pragmatic, fep021_efe_antitone_epistemic, and fep021_efe_eq_zero_iff.

### fep-022: Posterior-Predictive Kernel and Brier Properness

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep022.FEP022.fep022_brier_eq_optimum_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP022`.

Invariant: Markov posterior and likelihood kernels compose into normalized posterior-predictive fibres, and expected Bernoulli Brier loss is uniquely minimized by the true event probability.

Assumptions: Kernel normalization requires both components to be Markov. Brier properness is an exact real-algebra identity whose probabilistic interpretation restricts true and forecast masses to [0,1]. It is a proper one-event discrepancy, not an empirical calibration-frequency theorem.

Non-vacuity: Non-deterministic posterior and likelihood kernels produce nontrivial predictive true-event mass; every forecast q distinct from p incurs the strictly positive squared regret (q-p)^2.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep022_posteriorPredictive_mass_one, fep022_brier_decomposition, fep022_brier_eq_optimum_iff, and fep022_predictive_brier_decomposition.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep022_brier_eq_optimum_iff (p q : ℝ) :
    fep022_brierScore p q = p * (1 - p) ↔ q = p
```

### fep-023: Policy-Indexed Reachable Laws

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep023.FEP023.fep023_reachable_normalized`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP023`.

Invariant: The distributional affordance set is the image of available policies under their outcome-law map; policy-set inclusion is monotone and normalization of every policy law transfers to every reachable law.

Assumptions: Reachability is policy-indexed one-step image semantics. Normalization is an explicit premise on available policy laws; no controllability, multi-step support closure, or topology on the law space is claimed.

Non-vacuity: Distinct policies may induce distinct normalized measures, while an empty policy set yields no reachable law and a singleton set yields its single law.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep023_policy_reachable, fep023_reachable_mono, fep023_reachable_normalized, and fep023_reachable_empty.

### fep-024: KL Regularization in Objectives

Family: `core-information-geometry`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep024.FEP024.fep024_klRegularizedObjective_exact`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_information_geometry.py`; canonical namespace `FEP024`.

Invariant: An ENNReal objective regularized by a nonnegative weight times native measure KL upper-bounds its base, is monotone in weight, recovers the base at zero weight, and is exact when approximation equals a sigma-finite prior.

Assumptions: The codomain explicitly admits infinite objectives and KL values. Exactness at the prior uses sigma-finiteness; no differentiability or optimizer-existence claim is made.

Non-vacuity: Positive weight and unequal finite laws contribute a nonzero regularizer, while zero weight and matching laws independently exercise both exactness mechanisms.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep024_klRegularizedObjective_ge, fep024_klRegularizedObjective_zeroWeight, fep024_klRegularizedObjective_monoWeight, and fep024_klRegularizedObjective_exact.

### fep-025: Conserved Nonequilibrium Probability Currents

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep025.FEP025.fep025_cycleCurrent_stationary`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP025`.

Invariant: For flux πᵢPᵢⱼ, forward-minus-reverse current is antisymmetric; row normalization plus π-stationarity derives zero divergence. A directed three-state transition with uniform stationary law has a nonzero divergence-free current.

Assumptions: The general stationarity theorem explicitly requires normalized transition rows and a stationary real mass vector. The concrete witness is a finite deterministic cycle; no continuous-state NESS existence or thermodynamic force law is inferred here.

Non-vacuity: The clockwise three-state transition is nonidentity, preserves the uniform law, has current 1/3 on edge 0→1, and nevertheless has zero divergence at every state.

### fep-026: Negative-Log Prior Complexity

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep026.FEP026.fep026_priorComplexity_eq_zero_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP026`.

Invariant: Negative-log prior complexity is additive on positive product densities, strictly antitone, and zero exactly at unit density.

Assumptions: The input is a positive scalar prior-density value, not a normalized prior measure. Product additivity is exact; measure-valued relative complexity is owned by fep-014 and fep-024.

Non-vacuity: Every density in (0,1) has positive complexity, unit density is the unique positive zero, and unequal positive values exercise strict antitonicity.

### fep-027: Hierarchical Kernel Factorization

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep027.FEP027.fep027_hierarchical_assoc`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP027`.

Invariant: A parent measure and child kernel form a normalized hierarchical joint whose marginals recover the parent and prior predictive, and whose three-level factorization is associative up to product reassociation.

Assumptions: Joint normalization requires a probability parent and Markov child. First marginal requires an SFinite parent and Markov child; second marginal requires an SFinite parent and SFinite child. Associativity is the native compProd identity; no causal identifiability is imposed.

Non-vacuity: A non-Dirac finite parent and state-dependent non-deterministic Markov child give a dependent normalized joint with distinct parent and predictive marginals; a third kernel exercises nontrivial reassociation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep027_hierarchical_mass_one, fep027_hierarchical_fst, fep027_hierarchical_snd, and fep027_hierarchical_assoc.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
theorem fep027_hierarchical_assoc

    (fep027_hierarchicalJoint μ κ ⊗ₘ η).map MeasurableEquiv.prodAssoc =
```

### fep-028: Support-Aware Finite Softmax Policy

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep028.FEP028.fep028_softmax_sum_univ`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP028`.

Invariant: Exponentiated policy scores on a nonempty selected support define a nonnegative full finite weight vector, vanish outside support, are pointwise at most one, and sum to one globally.

Assumptions: Only support nonemptiness is required; inverse temperature and costs may be arbitrary reals. This proves an exact stochastic policy law but not optimality, sampling behavior, or a temperature limit.

Non-vacuity: With positive inverse temperature and a two-policy support of unequal costs, lower cost receives a strictly larger positive weight. At inverse temperature zero the law is uniform on support; excluded policies have zero mass. The full ten-policy vector remains normalized.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep028_softmax_nonneg, fep028_softmax_le_one, fep028_softmax_support, fep028_softmax_probs_sum_one, and fep028_softmax_sum_univ.

### fep-029: Quadratic Bregman Divergence

Family: `core-information-geometry`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep029.FEP029.fep029_quadraticBregman_eq_zero_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_information_geometry.py`; canonical namespace `FEP029`.

Invariant: The scalar Bregman divergence generated by φ(x)=x² equals (x-y)², is nonnegative, and vanishes exactly when x=y.

Assumptions: Unconditional over real x and y for the explicit quadratic generator; no general differentiable convex function, projection theorem, or mirror-descent convergence is claimed.

Non-vacuity: At x=1 and y=0 the divergence equals 1; at x=y it equals 0.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of the quadratic identity, nonnegativity, and zero-characterization theorems.

### fep-030: Maximum Entropy Principle

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep030.FEP030.fep030_binaryEntropy_max`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP030`.

Invariant: Binary Shannon entropy in nats is globally at most log 2, with equality exactly at probability one half.

Assumptions: The native Mathlib theorem is unconditional over real inputs because Real.binEntropy has a total extension; the scientific probability domain is [0,1]. This is the Bernoulli maximum-entropy principle, not the general constrained Jaynes problem.

Non-vacuity: The equality characterization proves attainment at p = 1/2 and strict loss at every other real input.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep030_binaryEntropy_max and fep030_binaryEntropy_eq_max_iff, plus the retained finite-uniform entropy calculation.

### fep-031: Finite Boltzmann–Gibbs Weights

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep031.FEP031.fep031_gibbsProbability_sum_one`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP031`.

Invariant: Positive exponential Gibbs weights normalize to a nonnegative mass function summing to one on any nonempty finite support.

Assumptions: Conditional only on a nonempty selected support; β and energies are arbitrary reals. The row does not derive Gibbs weights from maximum entropy or bundle the result as a measure. The expression sums to one only over the selected support and is not zero outside it. A support subtype or full support is required for an ambient normalized law.

Non-vacuity: A singleton selected support has mass one on that support. Two unequal energies give distinct weights when beta is nonzero, with lower energy receiving higher weight for positive beta; beta zero yields uniform supported weights.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of partition positivity, pointwise nonnegativity, and support normalization.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
    ∑ i ∈ S, fep031_gibbsProbability β n E S i = 1
```

### fep-032: Exact Quadratic Gradient-Descent Dynamics

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep032.FEP032.fep032_quadraticUpdate_tendsto`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP032`.

Invariant: Gradient descent on the centered scalar quadratic has an exact n-step iterate, nonincreasing energy for step sizes in [0,2], and converges globally to the center for steps in (0,2).

Assumptions: The closed form is unconditional. Energy descent uses 0≤η≤2 and convergence uses the strict stability interval 0<η<2. This is an exact quadratic model, not a theorem for arbitrary smooth or convex objectives.

Non-vacuity: At η=1/2 every displacement halves, producing nontrivial geometric convergence; η=1 reaches the center in one step and the endpoints expose the non-strict boundary.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep032_quadraticUpdate_deviation, fep032_quadraticUpdate_iterate, fep032_quadraticEnergy_descent, fep032_quadraticUpdate_tendsto, and FEPComposed.fep032_update_is_fep043_gradientStep.

### fep-033: Finite-Horizon Bellman Recursion

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep033.FEP033.fep033_bellman`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP033`.

Invariant: A deterministic transition-aware finite-horizon value obeys an exact Bellman recursion with stage cost, discount, and terminal cost; it is monotone in both cost functions and has exact zero-cost and zero-discount boundaries.

Assumptions: The transition is deterministic and the codomain is ENNReal. No stochastic policy optimization, discount-convergence limit, or infinite-horizon fixed point is claimed.

Non-vacuity: A nonconstant transition and positive discount propagate later-state terminal and stage costs, while zero discount isolates the immediate cost exactly.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep033_bellman, fep033_value_mono, fep033_zeroCost, and fep033_zeroDiscount.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep033_bellman {State : Type*}
    (discount : ENNReal) (stageCost : State → ENNReal)
    (step : State → State) (terminalCost : State → ENNReal)
    (horizon : ℕ) (state : State) :
    fep033_value discount stageCost step terminalCost (horizon + 1) state =
      stageCost state + discount *
        fep033_value discount stageCost step terminalCost horizon (step state)
```

### fep-034: Normalized Bayesian Filtering

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep034.FEP034.fep034_filter_joint_reconstruction`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP034`.

Invariant: A transition-composed predictive prior and native observation posterior reconstruct the swapped predicted-state/observation joint law, with probability mass one in every posterior fibre.

Assumptions: Conditional on standard-Borel nonempty latent structure, a finite prior, and finite transition and observation kernels; prediction preserves total mass when the transition is Markov, and posterior recovery additionally requires a Markov observation kernel. This is a one-step exact filter, not a convergence or learned-state theorem. Posterior normalization holds in every fibre of the selected Markov version; Bayes reconstruction identifies the version only relative to the predictive observation law, so no unique pointwise Bayes ratio at null observations is asserted.

Non-vacuity: A nonidentity finite Markov transition followed by a noisy observation kernel yields a nontrivial predictive prior and posterior; each posterior fibre has mass one rather than merely nonnegative unnormalized weight.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep034_predictive_mass, fep034_filter_mass_one, fep034_filter_joint_reconstruction, and fep034_filter_recovers_prediction.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep034_filter_joint_reconstruction
    (τ : Kernel Ω Ω) (κ : Kernel Ω 𝓧) (μ : Measure Ω)
    [StandardBorelSpace Ω] [Nonempty Ω]
    [IsFiniteMeasure μ] [IsFiniteKernel τ] [IsFiniteKernel κ] :
    (κ ∘ₘ fep034_predictivePrior τ μ) ⊗ₘ fep034_filter τ κ μ =
      (fep034_predictivePrior τ μ ⊗ₘ κ).map Prod.swap
```

### fep-035: Jensen's Inequality for Log

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep035.FEP035.fep035_log_jensen_two_strict`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP035`.

Invariant: For distinct positive inputs and strictly positive weights summing to one, the weighted average of logarithms is strictly below the logarithm of the weighted average.

Assumptions: Conditional on x,y > 0, x ≠ y, a,b > 0, and a+b=1. This is the exact two-point strict Jensen law; finite-family and measure-integral variants are not claimed.

Non-vacuity: Witnessed by x = 1, y = 2, and a = b = 1/2.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep035_log_strictConcave and fep035_log_jensen_two_strict with an interior-weight witness.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep035_log_jensen_two_strict
    {x y a b : ℝ} (hx : 0 < x) (hy : 0 < y) (hxy : x ≠ y)
    (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (hab : a + b = 1) :
    a * Real.log x + b * Real.log y < Real.log (a * x + b * y)
```

### fep-036: Laplace-Smoothed Empirical Bernoulli Prior

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep036.FEP036.fep036_smoothedRate_tendsto_of_empiricalRate`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP036`.

Invariant: A Mathlib finite binomial sampling law supplies admissible success counts; Laplace smoothing maps every outcome to an interior Bernoulli prior, is monotone in successes, and preserves the limit of any convergent empirical-frequency sequence.

Assumptions: The finite sampling law requires p in [0,1]. Interiority uses the intrinsic count bound, and consistency transfer explicitly assumes convergence of the raw empirical frequency. The row does not prove the binomial law of large numbers, a finite-sample risk bound, or marginal-likelihood hyperparameter optimality.

Non-vacuity: Every count in Fin (n+1) is admitted by the estimator; zero successes in zero trials gives 1/2, nontrivial counts vary within (0,1), and the shrinkage factor tends to one while the pseudocount offset tends to zero.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep036_binomialModel_apply, fep036_empiricalPrior_mem_Ioo, fep036_smoothedRate_mono, fep036_smoothedRate_eq_shrunkEmpirical, and fep036_smoothedRate_tendsto_of_empiricalRate, plus composed posterior closure.

### fep-037: Two-State Correlation–Response Law

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep037.FEP037.fep037_response_eq`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP037`.

Invariant: For the symmetric two-state relaxation eigenvalue 1-2α, stationary autocorrelation obeys an exact recurrence and decays for 0<α<1; the discrete fluctuation--response kernel equals 2βα variance times the same geometric mode and also decays.

Assumptions: Response is defined as inverse temperature times the one-step autocorrelation loss. Nonnegativity additionally restricts α≤1/2 and nonnegative inverse temperature and variance. This is an exact discrete two-state response law, not a microscopic Kubo derivation for general dynamics.

Non-vacuity: For alpha=1/4, positive beta and strictly positive variance, lag-zero response is positive and both defined correlation and response decay geometrically by1/2.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep037_autocorrelation_recurrence, fep037_autocorrelation_tendsto_zero, fep037_response_eq, fep037_response_nonneg, and fep037_response_tendsto_zero.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep037_response_eq (β α variance : ℝ) (n : ℕ) :
    fep037_response β α variance n =
      2 * β * α * variance * fep037_relaxation α ^ n
```
