### fep-038: Bernoulli Fisher Information and Natural Gradient

Family: `core-information-geometry`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep038.FEP038.fep038_fisherInformation_eq`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_information_geometry.py`; canonical namespace `FEP038`.

Invariant: The differentiated Bernoulli family has zero expected score and Fisher information 1/(p(1-p)); its inverse metric defines a dual natural gradient. The supplied coordinate-Jacobian expression squares to Fisher information and factorizes the metric.

Assumptions: Metric positivity, inverse-metric duality and the coordinate-Jacobian algebra require 0 < p < 1. Identification of that supplied expression with the derivative of the authored 2*arcsin(sqrt p) coordinate requires the separately reviewed interior HasDerivAt bridge. No path-length theorem or general statistical manifold is asserted by the current topic declarations.

Non-vacuity: At p = 1/2 the Fisher information is 4, every nonzero tangent has positive metric norm, and the natural-gradient map rescales nonzero covectors by 1/4.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep038_bernoulliMass_hasDerivAt, fep038_expectedScore_zero, fep038_fisherInformation_eq, fep038_fisherMetric_pos, fep038_naturalGradient_duality, and fep038_fisherMetric_coordinate.

### fep-040: Native Gaussian Law and Thermal Entropy Response

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep040.FEP040.fep040_thermalEntropy_hasDerivAt`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP040`.

Invariant: Mathlib's real Gaussian law is normalized with its stated mean and variance; the one-dimensional closed-form entropy is strictly variance-monotone, differentiable with derivative 1/(2v), and under v=κT has derivative 1/(2T) and heat capacity 1/2.

Assumptions: The native probability, mean, and variance theorems include the zero-variance Dirac boundary. Entropy monotonicity and derivatives require positive variance, and the thermal relation requires κ,T>0. The closed-form entropy is specified directly rather than rederived here as an integral of negative log density.

Non-vacuity: For unit scale and positive temperature, the Gaussian law is nondegenerate, its entropy response is nonzero, and the dimensionless heat capacity is exactly 1/2.

### fep-041: Expected Information Gain via Measure KL

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep041.FEP041.fep041_expectedInformationGain_zero`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP041`.

Invariant: Information gain is native measure-valued KL divergence: it is nonnegative, separates finite posterior and prior measures, and its predictive expectation vanishes when observations leave the posterior unchanged almost everywhere.

Assumptions: KL separation requires finite measures. The zero expected-information theorem requires a sigma-finite prior and almost-everywhere posterior equality; it does not claim that every informative experiment has strictly positive expected gain.

Non-vacuity: An observation-independent posterior equal to the prior yields zero gain, while any unequal finite posterior/prior pair has nonzero KL by separation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep041_informationGain_nonneg, fep041_informationGain_eq_zero_iff, and fep041_expectedInformationGain_zero.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose.
```lean
theorem fep041_expectedInformationGain_zero
    (predictive : Measure Obs)
    (posterior : Obs → Measure State)
    (prior : Measure State) [SigmaFinite prior]
    (hposterior : ∀ᵐ observation ∂predictive, posterior observation = prior) :
    fep041_expectedInformationGain predictive posterior prior = 0
```

### fep-042: Bernoulli Sufficient-Statistic Factorization

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep042.FEP042.fep042_likelihood_eq_of_stat_eq`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP042`.

Invariant: The Bernoulli sequence likelihood factorizes exactly through success and failure counts, so samples with equal count statistics have identical likelihood for every parameter.

Assumptions: The theorem is finite-sample and algebraic over a Boolean observation list. Its probabilistic interpretation restricts p to [0,1]; no completeness, asymptotic efficiency, or non-Bernoulli family is claimed.

Non-vacuity: Distinct orderings such as [true,false] and [false,true] have the same statistic and likelihood, while samples with different success counts generally differ for interior p.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep042_likelihood_factorizes and fep042_likelihood_eq_of_stat_eq on permuted Boolean samples.

### fep-043: Fermat Criticality and Quadratic Curvature

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep043.FEP043.fep043_quadratic_critical_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP043`.

Invariant: Fermat's theorem makes every local minimum critical; for an exact positive-curvature quadratic, the derivative is the stated gradient, the center is the unique critical point and global minimizer, and the scalar Hessian is strictly positive.

Assumptions: The general derivative-zero theorem uses Mathlib's local-minimum notion. Uniqueness and positive second derivative are proved only for the explicit scalar quadratic with a>0, not inferred for arbitrary differentiable objectives. The general deriv = 0 wrapper uses Mathlib's totalized derivative, which is zero also at nondifferentiable points; only the explicit quadratic HasDerivAt declarations establish differentiability here.

Non-vacuity: Every positive a and noncenter x has nonzero gradient and strictly larger energy than the center, while the Hessian 2a is nonzero everywhere.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep043_localMin_deriv_zero, fep043_quadratic_hasDerivAt, fep043_quadratic_unique_min, fep043_quadratic_critical_iff, and fep043_quadratic_hessian_pos.

### fep-045: Finite Bernoulli Conjugate Closure

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep045.FEP045.fep045_bernoulli_posterior_closed`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP045`.

Invariant: For nonzero evidence, likelihood-times-prior masses have the Bernoulli mass-vector form at the updated parameter. A probability-law interpretation additionally requires a prior in [0,1], nonnegative likelihoods and positive evidence.

Assumptions: Closure is exact for a binary latent hypothesis and binary evidence likelihood. Interior prior and positive likelihood assumptions prove positive evidence and keep the updated parameter in [0,1]. This is a finite Bernoulli conjugate family, not Beta-density conjugacy.

Non-vacuity: Unequal positive likelihoods move an interior prior parameter while posterior mass still sums to one. Equal nonzero likelihoods preserve the prior; both zero expose the refused evidence boundary.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep045_evidence_pos, fep045_posteriorParameter_mem_unit, fep045_bernoulli_posterior_closed, and fep045_posterior_mass_one.

This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
theorem fep045_bernoulli_posterior_closed
    {p l₀ l₁ : ℝ} (hZ : fep045_evidence p l₀ l₁ ≠ 0) (b : Bool) :
    fep045_bernoulliMass (fep045_posteriorParameter p l₀ l₁) b =
      fep045_bernoulliMass p b * fep045_binaryLikelihood l₀ l₁ b /
        fep045_evidence p l₀ l₁
```

### fep-046: Finite Stick-Breaking Mass Conservation

Family: `core-bayesian-mechanics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep046.FEP046.fep046_mass_conservation`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_bayesian_mechanics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP046`.

Invariant: For every finite stick-breaking sequence, allocated weights plus residual mass equal exactly one; unit-interval breaks keep the residual in [0,1].

Assumptions: Mass conservation is a purely algebraic identity with no bounds. Residual nonnegativity and the unit upper bound require every break fraction in [0,1]. No infinite-series exhaustion or random-process law is claimed.

Non-vacuity: Multiple unequal interior breaks allocate several positive weights and a positive remainder, while a break of one exhausts all subsequent residual mass.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep046_mass_conservation, fep046_remainder_nonneg, and fep046_remainder_le_one on empty, singleton, and multi-break lists.

### fep-047: Compositional Finite Sum-Product Propagation

Family: `core-active-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep047.FEP047.fep047_forward_compose`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_active_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP047`.

Invariant: Finite sum-product forward propagation is matrix--vector multiplication: it preserves nonnegativity and order under nonnegative factors, has zero and identity laws, and composes exactly by matrix multiplication.

Assumptions: Nonnegativity preservation requires nonnegative factors and inputs. Order monotonicity requires nonnegative factors and pointwise ordered inputs, which may themselves be negative. Identity/composition are unconditional algebraic laws. The row proves exact finite propagation, not normalization or loopy fixed-point convergence.

Non-vacuity: Nonidentity dense matrices and nonconstant incoming vectors exercise genuine cross-state aggregation; two distinct factors exercise nontrivial composed propagation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep047_forward_nonneg, fep047_forward_mono, fep047_zero_in, fep047_identity, and fep047_forward_compose using Mathlib Matrix.mulVec_mulVec.

### fep-048: Contractive Policy Update Uniqueness

Family: `core-free-energy`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_free_energy.py`; canonical namespace `FEP048`.

### fep-049: Linear Constitutive Entropy Production

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep049.FEP049.fep049_entropyProduction_eq_zero_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP049`.

Invariant: Under Jᵢ=LᵢXᵢ, the flux--force pairing is a nonnegative quadratic form that separates equilibrium for Lᵢ>0. Independently, positive forward/reverse edge fluxes have production (f-r)log(f/r)≥0, with equality exactly at detailed balance.

Assumptions: The finite constitutive law is diagonal and assumes nonnegative conductances. The edge theorem assumes strictly positive bidirectional fluxes so affinity is finite. Cross-coupled Onsager matrices and microscopic derivations remain outside the claim.

Non-vacuity: Any nonzero force under positive conductance produces positive quadratic entropy; unequal positive directed fluxes produce positive edge entropy, while equality of fluxes is the exact zero-production boundary.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep049_flux_force_identity, fep049_entropyProduction_nonneg, fep049_entropyProduction_eq_zero_iff, fep049_edgeProduction_nonneg, and fep049_edgeProduction_eq_zero_iff.

```lean
      ∀ i, force i = 0
```

### fep-050: Logical Erasure and the Landauer Bound

Family: `core-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep050.FEP050.fep050_landauer_work_bound`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/core_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP050`.

Invariant: A normalized finite path erasure model with forward-supported local detailed balance derives mean bath heat at least kB T times the prior's binary Shannon entropy when the final bit is reset. The fair-prior corner gives kB T log 2, and work dominating that mean heat obeys the same threshold. No second-law inequality is assumed by these path-model bounds.

Assumptions: ErasureModel supplies finite normalized forward and aligned reverse path laws, initial and final marginals, positive beta, reverse support on every forward-supported path, and local detailed balance. The primary work theorem additionally requires positive kB and T, beta = 1/(kB T), reset to false, prior(true) = 1/2, and meanHeat <= W. The generalized prior endpoint assumes the same beta identification and reset but does not separately require positive kB and T; the model forces their product positive. Shannon entropy and mean path production are dimensionless nats: meanHeat = kB T sigma + kB T (H(prior) - H(final)). For physical entropy loss DeltaS = kB DeltaH, the latter term is T DeltaS. Local detailed balance and work/heat domination are modeling assumptions, not a microscopic derivation, first-law proof, finite-time realization, laboratory result, or causal claim. The separately named entropy-balance helpers remain conditional and do not construct a path protocol.

Non-vacuity: reversibleErasureWitness constructs a normalized reset with aligned reverse law equal to the forward law, derived zero production, and heat equal to binEntropy(prior(true))/beta. At kB = 2 and T = 3 the fair witness attains 6 log 2; the actual prior(true) = 3/4 witness attains its generalized bound strictly below the fair threshold. Equality is proved for these mathematical witnesses rather than supplied as a model field or claimed for an apparatus.

Canonical acceptance probe: Warning-strict native Lean compile and axiom inspection of the focused and aggregate generalized, fair heat, primary work, and conditional entropy-balance helpers; the existing full core composition must compile with its explicit conditional helper endpoint. Imported maintained controls must verify heat encoding, fair bound, reversible equality, biased bound, strict biased-versus-fair boundary, and work domination at the same positive scale. Dropping reset, beta identification, or local detailed balance must not be accepted as the path theorem. Current-source qualification remains separate from this semantic review and from historical external compile receipts.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
theorem fep050_landauer_work_bound
    {Path : Type*} [Fintype Path]
    (model : FEP.PathThermodynamics.ErasureModel Path)
    {W kB T : ℝ} (hkB : 0 < kB) (hT : 0 < T)
    (hBeta : model.beta = (kB * T)⁻¹)
    (hReset : model.IsReset) (hFair : model.prior true = 1 / 2)
    (hworkHeat : model.meanHeat ≤ W) :
    fep050_landauerBound kB T ≤ W
```

### fep-051: Likelihood-Ratio Reconstruction under Absolute Continuity

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep051.FEP051.fep051_likelihoodRatio_reconstruction`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/measure_bayesian_inversion.py`; canonical namespace `FEP051`.

Invariant: A native Radon–Nikodym derivative reconstructs a target measure from its reference exactly when the target is absolutely continuous with respect to that reference.

Assumptions: Both results require a measurable carrier and a HaveLebesgueDecomposition instance. The forward reconstruction additionally assumes target ≪ reference; the equivalence proves that this is the exact support condition and does not erase a singular component.

Non-vacuity: Equal finite reference/target laws supply a concrete reconstructible case with the required decomposition instance; a nonzero singular component gives the explicit failure boundary.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep051_likelihoodRatio_reconstruction and fep051_reconstruction_iff_absoluteContinuous with the absolute-continuity premise visible.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. theorem fep051_likelihoodRatio_reconstruction
```lean
    (target reference : Measure Ω)
    [target.HaveLebesgueDecomposition reference]
    (h_ac : target ≪ reference) :
    reference.withDensity (target.rnDeriv reference) = target
```

### fep-052: Posterior Density as a Likelihood Tilt

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep052.FEP052.fep052_posterior_density_tilt`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/measure_bayesian_inversion.py`; canonical namespace `FEP052`.

Invariant: A posterior kernel is a prior measure tilted by the likelihood-to-predictive Radon–Nikodym derivative for predictive-almost-every observation.

Assumptions: The native result assumes a finite prior, a finite likelihood kernel, a nonempty standard-Borel parameter space, countable generation between the parameter and observation spaces, and prior-almost-every likelihood domination. The countable specialization discharges domination but remains an almost-everywhere identity, not a pointwise formula on null observations.

Non-vacuity: Countable nonempty parameter spaces and finite Markov likelihoods instantiate the specialization without an authored domination proof; observations in a predictive null set remain intentionally unconstrained.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep052_posterior_density_tilt and fep052_countable_posterior_density_tilt, preserving predictive-a.e. equality.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep052_posterior_density_tilt
    (prior : Measure Parameter) [IsFiniteMeasure prior]
    (likelihood : Kernel Parameter Observation) [IsFiniteKernel likelihood]
    (h_ac : ∀ᵐ parameter ∂prior,
      likelihood parameter ≪ likelihood ∘ₘ prior) :
    ∀ᵐ observation ∂likelihood ∘ₘ prior,
      (likelihood†prior) observation =
        prior.withDensity (fun parameter =>
          likelihood.rnDeriv
            (Kernel.const Parameter (likelihood ∘ₘ prior))
            parameter observation)
```

Namespace variable context:

```lean
{Parameter Observation : Type*}
```

### fep-053: Kernel Bayesian-Inversion Reconstruction

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep053.FEP053.fep053_kernelBayes_joint_reconstruction`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/measure_bayesian_inversion.py`; canonical namespace `FEP053`.

Invariant: Bayesian inversion reconstructs the swapped prior-likelihood joint measure, and at every positive finite evidence atom posterior mass times evidence equals the corresponding joint atom.

Assumptions: The measure theorem requires a finite prior and likelihood on a nonempty standard-Borel parameter space. The finite atom theorem additionally exposes strictly positive predictive evidence; it makes no normalized posterior claim at a zero-evidence atom.

Non-vacuity: Any informative finite likelihood with a positive predictive atom exercises the atom reconstruction, while the measure equality covers the complete swapped joint rather than only one selected observation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep053_kernelBayes_joint_reconstruction and fep053_finiteBayes_atom_reconstruction with positive evidence explicit.

```lean
theorem fep053_kernelBayes_joint_reconstruction
    (prior : Measure Parameter) [IsFiniteMeasure prior]
    (likelihood : Kernel Parameter Observation) [IsFiniteKernel likelihood] :
    (likelihood ∘ₘ prior) ⊗ₘ likelihood†prior =
      (prior ⊗ₘ likelihood).map Prod.swap
```

### fep-054: Bayes Involution for Finite Joint Laws

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep054.FEP054.fep054_bayes_involution`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/measure_bayesian_inversion.py`; canonical namespace `FEP054`.

Invariant: Inverting a finite Markov likelihood twice returns the original likelihood prior-almost-everywhere, and one inversion followed by prediction reconstructs the prior exactly as a measure.

Assumptions: The likelihood and prior are native finite kernels and measures, not only finite atom tables. Involution requires both parameter and observation carriers to be nonempty standard Borel and the likelihood to be Markov; its conclusion is almost-everywhere rather than pointwise kernel equality.

Non-vacuity: Every finite Markov likelihood on nonempty standard-Borel spaces satisfies exact prior reconstruction, while distinct versions of a posterior on null observations explain why involution is stated almost everywhere.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep054_bayes_involution and fep054_posterior_reconstructs_prior, retaining the prior-a.e. boundary.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose.     (prior : Measure Parameter) [IsFiniteMeasure prior]
```lean
    (likelihood : Kernel Parameter Observation)
    [StandardBorelSpace Observation] [Nonempty Observation]
    [IsFiniteKernel likelihood] [IsMarkovKernel likelihood] :
    (likelihood†prior)†(likelihood ∘ₘ prior) =ᵐ[prior] likelihood
```

### fep-055: Composite-Kernel Bayesian Inversion

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep055.FEP055.fep055_compositeKernel_bayesInversion`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/measure_bayesian_inversion.py`; canonical namespace `FEP055`.

Invariant: Bayesian inversion reverses a two-kernel composition into the corresponding composition of inversions, and the associated predictive law is exactly the chronological two-stage prediction.

Assumptions: The prior and both kernels are finite, and the parameter and intermediate spaces are nonempty standard Borel. The inverse-kernel equality holds only almost everywhere under the final predictive law; predictive associativity itself is an unconditional measure equality under finiteness.

Non-vacuity: Two nonidentity finite kernels produce a genuine intermediate marginal and final predictive law, while final-predictive null observations retain the expected version ambiguity.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep055_compositeKernel_bayesInversion and fep055_compositePredictive_associativity with the final predictive a.e. filter.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
theorem fep055_compositeKernel_bayesInversion
    (prior : Measure Parameter) [IsFiniteMeasure prior]
    [StandardBorelSpace Parameter] [Nonempty Parameter]
    [StandardBorelSpace Intermediate] [Nonempty Intermediate]
    (earlier : Kernel Parameter Intermediate) [IsFiniteKernel earlier]
    (later : Kernel Intermediate Observation) [IsFiniteKernel later] :
    (later ∘ₖ earlier)†prior =ᵐ[later ∘ₘ earlier ∘ₘ prior]
      earlier†prior ∘ₖ later†(earlier ∘ₘ prior)
```

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Invariant: Mathlib's canonical conditional kernel reconstructs a finite joint measure from its first marginal and is normalized at every conditioning value.

Assumptions: The conditioned carrier must be nonempty standard Borel and the joint measure finite. This is the maintained standard-Borel disintegration theorem, not a claim that regular conditional probabilities exist on arbitrary measurable spaces.

### fep-057: Conditional-Expectation Tower through a Kernel

Family: `measure-bayesian-inversion`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep057.FEP057.fep057_conditionalExpectation_tower`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/measure_bayesian_inversion.py`; canonical namespace `FEP057`.

Invariant: Integrating a conditional expectation against the conditioning marginal equals the original joint expectation, with a parallel extended-nonnegative tower law.

Assumptions: The joint measure is finite and the conditioned carrier nonempty standard Borel. Real-valued observables must be integrable; extended-nonnegative observables need measurability but no separate integrability premise.

Non-vacuity: Dependent finite joints and nonconstant observables exercise genuine nested integration, while nonintegrable signed observables are excluded rather than assigned a spurious finite expectation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep057_conditionalExpectation_tower and fep057_conditionalLIntegral_tower with their distinct observable hypotheses.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep057_conditionalExpectation_tower
    (joint : Measure (Conditioning × Conditioned)) [IsFiniteMeasure joint]
    {observable : Conditioning × Conditioned → ℝ}
    (hintegrable : Integrable observable joint) :
    (∫ conditioning,
        ∫ conditioned, observable (conditioning, conditioned)
          ∂joint.condKernel conditioning ∂joint.fst) =
      ∫ pair, observable pair ∂joint
```

### fep-058: Finite Gibbs Variational Principle

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep058.FEP058.fep058_gibbsVariational_lower_bound`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP058`.

Invariant: Finite Gibbs free energy is bounded below by minus the certified log partition, and the explicit normalized Gibbs law attains that bound.

Assumptions: GibbsCertificate exposes strict support for reference and optimizer laws and the exact pointwise log-density identity. The theorem is finite and does not assert existence of such a certificate for arbitrary continuous potentials.

Non-vacuity: On every nonempty finite carrier, the uniform zero-potential certificate is full support and attains objective value zero, demonstrating compatible normalization and logarithmic assumptions.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep058_gibbsVariational_lower_bound, fep058_gibbsVariational_optimizer, and fep058_uniformGibbs_nonvacuity.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

```lean
theorem fep058_gibbsVariational_lower_bound
    (certificate : GibbsCertificate State) (candidate : FiniteLaw State) :
    -certificate.logPartition ≤
      gibbsFreeEnergy certificate candidate
```

### fep-059: Finite Donsker–Varadhan Equality with a Full-Support Gibbs Optimizer

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep059.FEP059.fep059_donskerVaradhan_equality_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP059`.

Invariant: The finite Donsker–Varadhan objective is bounded by the certified log partition, with equality exactly at the supplied full-support Gibbs optimizer.

Assumptions: The result is parameterized by GibbsCertificate, whose strict-support and log-density fields make the finite logarithmic boundary explicit. It does not extend the equality to arbitrary measures, unbounded potentials, or an optimizer whose existence has not been certified.

Non-vacuity: The uniform zero-potential certificate from the shared foundation supplies a normalized full-support optimizer; unequal candidates have a strictly positive KL gap and therefore cannot attain equality.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep059_donskerVaradhan_upper_bound, fep059_donskerVaradhan_optimizer, and fep059_donskerVaradhan_equality_iff.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep059_donskerVaradhan_equality_iff
    (certificate : GibbsCertificate State) (candidate : FiniteLaw State) :
    dvObjective certificate candidate = certificate.logPartition ↔
      candidate = certificate.optimizer
```

### fep-060: Coordinate ELBO Decomposition

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep060.FEP060.fep060_coordinateELBO_decomposition`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP060`.

Invariant: A finite joint ELBO separates exactly into a prior-coordinate score minus an expected conditional KL remainder, and that remainder is nonnegative.

Assumptions: The decomposition assumes every reference-prior and reference-kernel atom is strictly positive so all logarithmic rewrites are sound. Conditional KL nonnegativity is proved for the project's totalized finite divergence, but no zero-reference extended-real interpretation is claimed.

Non-vacuity: If actual and reference rows differ at a latent state of positive actual-prior mass, the conditional remainder is positive. Differences confined to zero-prior rows need not contribute. Matching kernels make this coordinate penalty zero while leaving the prior score visible.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep060_coordinateELBO_decomposition and fep060_coordinateKL_nonnegative with reference support hypotheses exposed.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep060_coordinateELBO_decomposition
    (actualPrior referencePrior : FiniteLaw Latent)
    (actualKernel referenceKernel : FiniteKernel Latent Observation)
    (hprior : ∀ latent, 0 < referencePrior latent)
    (hkernel : ∀ latent observation,
      0 < referenceKernel latent observation) :
    jointELBO actualPrior referencePrior actualKernel referenceKernel =
      -finiteKL actualPrior referencePrior -
        conditionalKL actualPrior actualKernel referenceKernel
```

### fep-061: Mean-Field Coordinate Free-Energy Optimum

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep061.FEP061.fep061_meanFieldCoordinate_optimum_iff`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP061`.

Invariant: Holding one supported mean-field factor fixed reduces the coordinate free energy exactly to finite KL, whose unique zero is the supported target factor.

Assumptions: The fixed and target laws have full support on finite carriers. The theorem is a one-coordinate optimum with the other factor held fixed; it does not prove global convergence of alternating mean-field updates or handle continuous variational families.

Non-vacuity: A candidate different from the full-support target has strictly positive coordinate free energy, whereas selecting the target attains the exact zero optimum for any full-support fixed factor.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep061_meanFieldCoordinate_reduction and fep061_meanFieldCoordinate_optimum_iff with both support premises.

### fep-062: Fixed-Sample Importance-Weighted Jensen Bound

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep062.FEP062.fep062_iidProduct_importanceJensen`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP062`.

Invariant: For a positive fixed sample count and positive weights, the IID product-law expectation of the log sample-mean weight is at most the log of its expected sample-mean weight.

Assumptions: The carrier is finite, NeZero excludes sample count zero, and every weight is strictly positive before Real.log is used. The positive-expectation witness additionally assumes a nonempty carrier and full-support sampling law.

Non-vacuity: A sampling law assigning positive probability to two different weight values gives a genuinely random sample mean. Nonconstant weights off the sampling support do not. A one-sample law recovers finite weighted Jensen.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep062_iidProduct_importanceJensen, fep062_fixedSample_importanceJensen, and fep062_expectedImportanceWeight_positive for positive weights.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep062_iidProduct_importanceJensen
    (base : FiniteLaw Sample) (sampleCount : ℕ) [NeZero sampleCount]
    (weight : Sample → ℝ) (hweight : ∀ sample, 0 < weight sample) :
    ∑ sampleTuple, iidProductLaw base sampleCount sampleTuple *
        Real.log (sampleMeanWeight sampleCount weight sampleTuple) ≤
      Real.log (∑ sampleTuple, iidProductLaw base sampleCount sampleTuple *
        sampleMeanWeight sampleCount weight sampleTuple)
```

### fep-063: Finite-Channel KL Data-Processing Inequality

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep063.FEP063.fep063_finiteChannel_klDataProcessing_of_relativeSupport`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP063`.

Invariant: Passing finite actual and reference laws through the same finite channel cannot increase finite KL when every atom carrying nonzero actual mass has strictly positive reference mass. Shared-zero atoms and zero channel entries are permitted.

Assumptions: The selected primary exposes relative input support explicitly; it does not assume strict positivity of either full input law or all channel entries. Local discrete measurable instances bridge the existing finite-law theorem. This is the project's totalized real finiteKL convention under relative support, not a theorem identifying a singular native extended KL with a finite real value. The retained former primary still provides its exact full-support specialization and unchanged API.

Non-vacuity: Unequal full-support laws exercise the positive-support specialization. A point mass compared with a diagonal reference law has genuine shared-zero atoms and passes a deterministic projection through the relative-support theorem. Identity preserves finite KL and complete coarsening contracts it. Disjoint point masses violate the support premise: their totalized finiteKL is one while native extended KL is infinite.

Canonical acceptance probe: Strict native Lean compile of the selected relative-support wrapper and retained old primary, plus imported full-support, sparse/shared-zero, deterministic projection, identity and singular support/native-infinity controls. Require exact standard-only axiom reports and preserve the existing coarsening boundary. Compilation does not itself promote semantic disposition. The strict imported-consumer command, from lean/, is `lake env lean -DwarningAsError=true ../specs/comprehensive-next-steps/spikes/primary-alignment-20261022/Consumers.lean`. After official generator synchronization, first require a successful current-source `lake build --wfail FepSketches` from lean/ with pinned Lean 4.35; the imported consumer must not use stale prior-build objects. Its 13 exact axiom queries comprise the selected canonical wrapper and 12 consumer statements, not 13 independent compiler experiments. The unchanged former primary and catalog boundaries also require the actual native catalogue/API and declaration-audit gates.

### fep-064: Rate–Distortion Lagrangian Weak Duality

Family: `variational-duality-and-information-bounds`. Disposition: `structural_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep064.FEP064.fep064_rateDistortion_dual_lower_bound`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/variational_duality.py`; canonical namespace `FEP064`.

Invariant: The actual finite rate-distortion Lagrangian-infimum dual value is at most the mutual information of every joint law with the required source marginal and distortion budget, for nonnegative distortion and multiplier. The former component-bound Lagrangian inequality remains an unchanged supporting API.

Assumptions: The selected primary assumes an actual feasible finite joint and nonnegative distortion and multiplier; it does not assume separately certified component lower bounds. The underlying infimum is genuinely derived. Retained support separately provides primal minimizer attainment by compactness. The reviewed finite F07 capability proves primal minimum equals nonnegative-multiplier dual supremum for every feasible finite problem, and finite dual attainment with complementary slackness under strict Slater feasibility. Those scoped capabilities do not turn this primary weak bound into unrestricted general strong duality, finite boundary dual attainment, or optimizer uniqueness. The structural-proxy disposition remains unchanged pending explicit review.

Non-vacuity: A feasible fair Boolean quarter-budget joint has positive mutual information and exercises the weak bound at a positive multiplier; this does not assert that the chosen multiplier maximizes the dual. Zero multiplier is a separate boundary with zero dual value on a nonempty code alphabet. Negative budgets are infeasible for nonnegative distortion; zero budget forces a unique Boolean diagonal joint while half budget has distinct information minimizers.

Canonical acceptance probe: Strict native Lean compile of the existing actual-infimum weak-dual primary, former component-bound primary and retained minimizer/informative-interior support, plus positive-multiplier, zero-dual, negative-budget, nonunique-half and unique-zero controls. Preserve the separate finite F07 all-feasible dual-supremum and Slater-attainment scope and zero-source-atom controls. Exact declaration ownership and standard-only axiom reports are required. The same strict imported-consumer command from lean/ is `lake env lean -DwarningAsError=true ../specs/comprehensive-next-steps/spikes/primary-alignment-20261022/Consumers.lean`. After official generator synchronization, first require a successful current-source `lake build --wfail FepSketches` from lean/ with pinned Lean 4.35; the imported consumer must not use stale prior-build objects. Its 13 queries cover the canonical 063 wrapper and 12 shared consumer statements, including the actual 064 weak bound and multiplier controls. Retained old-primary and additional 064 boundaries remain subject to the actual native catalogue/API and declaration-audit gates separately.

Invariant: A positive-evidence action-conditioned prediction and observation update is normalized and reconstructs its predicted state-observation joint mass.

Assumptions: All carriers are finite and the selected action has strictly positive observation evidence. The normalized posterior is not constructed at zero evidence, and the theorem concerns one update rather than policy optimality.

Non-vacuity: Informative emissions after distinct action-conditioned transitions yield different posterior beliefs; the zero-evidence theorem pins the exact point where the normalized construction is unavailable.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep066_actionConditioned_bayes_reconstruction, fep066_actionConditioned_update_normalized, and fep066_zero_evidence_boundary.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep066_actionConditioned_bayes_reconstruction
    (prior : FiniteLaw State) (transition : ControlledKernel State Action)
    (emission : FiniteKernel State Observation) (action : Action)
    (observation : Observation)
    (hEvidence : 0 < actionEvidence prior transition emission action observation)
    (state : State) :
    actionBeliefUpdate prior transition emission action observation hEvidence state *
        actionEvidence prior transition emission action observation =
      actionPrediction prior transition action state * emission state observation
```

### fep-067: Reachable Finite-Belief POMDP Reduction

Family: `control-and-planning-as-inference`. Disposition: `structural_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep067.FEP067.fep067_reachableBelief_policyValue_equivalence`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/controlled_markov.py`; canonical namespace `FEP067`.

Invariant: Expanding a finite reachable-belief index into its interpreted finite law preserves every fixed finite-horizon feedback-policy value.

Assumptions: ReachableBeliefPOMDP supplies a finite indexed carrier, an interpretation into FiniteLaw, and sound positive-evidence updates; DecidableEq is required for the belief index. This is not a finite enumeration of the full probability simplex or an infinite-horizon POMDP reduction.

Non-vacuity: The concrete Boolean reachable model proves an exact positive-evidence Bayes update between two belief indexes, and arbitrary horizons exercise recursive policy-value preservation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep067_reachableBelief_policyValue_equivalence and fep067_bool_reachable_update on the finite indexed belief carrier.

```lean
theorem fep067_reachableBelief_policyValue_equivalence
    (model : ReachableBeliefPOMDP Belief State Action Observation)
    (stateCost : State → Action → ℝ) (policy : ℕ → Belief → Action)
    (horizon : ℕ) (belief : Belief) :
```

### fep-068: Soft Bellman Recursion

Family: `control-and-planning-as-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep068.FEP068.fep068_softBellman_recursion`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/controlled_markov.py`; canonical namespace `FEP068`.

Invariant: On a nonempty finite action carrier, a positive-temperature soft Bellman partition is positive, its successor value is the exact temperature-scaled log-sum-exp backup, and that soft value is no larger than any hard action energy.

Assumptions: State and action sets are finite, the action carrier is inhabited, and temperature is strictly positive before division, logarithmic aggregation, and the soft-minimum comparison. The statement is an exact finite-horizon recursion and pointwise hard/soft bound, not an infinite-horizon fixed-point or optimality-limit theorem.

Non-vacuity: Inhabited actions make the exponential partition strictly positive; nonconstant costs and action-dependent transition rows make multiple exponentiated action values contribute to the finite soft minimum.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep068_softBellman_recursion with a nonempty action carrier and positive temperature, including its positive-partition, successor-recursion, and actionwise hard/soft conclusions.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep068_softBellman_recursion (temperature : ℝ)
    (hTemperature : 0 < temperature) (stageCost : State → Action → ℝ)
    (transition : ControlledKernel State Action)
    (horizon : ℕ) (state : State) :
    (0 < ∑ action,
      Real.exp (-((softBellmanActionEnergy temperature stageCost transition
        horizon state action) / temperature))) ∧
    (softBellmanValue temperature stageCost transition (horizon + 1) state =
      -temperature * Real.log
        (∑ action,
          Real.exp (-((softBellmanActionEnergy temperature stageCost transition
            horizon state action) / temperature)))) ∧
    ∀ action,
      softBellmanValue temperature stageCost transition (horizon + 1) state ≤
        softBellmanActionEnergy temperature stageCost transition
          horizon state action
```

### fep-069: KL-Control Desirability Recursion

Family: `control-and-planning-as-inference`. Disposition: `structural_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep069.FEP069.fep069_desirability_recursion`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/controlled_markov.py`; canonical namespace `FEP069`.

Invariant: One finite desirability backup is an exponential state-cost tilt of a passive transition prediction and preserves nonnegativity.

Assumptions: The theorem gives the algebraic KL-control desirability operator on a finite Markov kernel. It does not derive this operator from a path-space KL objective, prove an optimal controlled kernel, or establish convergence of iteration.

Non-vacuity: Nonzero costs and nonconstant nonnegative desirability produce a nontrivial tilted prediction, while zero cost and unit desirability form an exact normalized fixed point.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep069_desirability_recursion, fep069_desirability_nonnegative, and fep069_zeroCost_unitDesirability.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose.
```lean
theorem fep069_desirability_recursion
    (passive : FiniteKernel State State) (stateCost desirability : State → ℝ)
    (state : State) :
    desirabilityStep passive stateCost desirability state =
      Real.exp (-stateCost state) *
        ∑ nextState, passive state nextState * desirability nextState
```

### fep-070: Control-as-Inference Policy Posterior

Family: `control-and-planning-as-inference`. Disposition: `structural_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep070.FEP070.fep070_controlPosterior_reconstruction`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/controlled_markov.py`; canonical namespace `FEP070`.

Invariant: A prior-weighted exponential action score normalizes to a control posterior whose mass reconstructs the unnormalized score.

Assumptions: The action carrier is finite, precision and costs are real, and positivity of the exponential partition is supplied by the finite Boltzmann construction. The result is an exact algebraic posterior but does not derive its cost from a full generative observation model.

Non-vacuity: For nonzero precision and two positive-prior actions with distinct costs, their posterior odds differ from prior odds. Zero precision or constant supported costs preserves the prior; zero cost does so for every precision.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep070_controlPosterior_normalized, fep070_controlPosterior_reconstruction, and fep070_zeroCost_posterior_eq_prior.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep070_controlPosterior_reconstruction (prior : FiniteLaw Action)
    (precision : ℝ) (cost : Action → ℝ) (action : Action) :
    controlPosterior prior precision cost action *
        boltzmannPartition prior (fun candidate => precision * cost candidate) =
      boltzmannWeight prior (fun candidate => precision * cost candidate) action
```

### fep-071: Sophisticated Expected-Free-Energy Backward Induction

Family: `control-and-planning-as-inference`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep071.FEP071.fep071_sophisticatedEFE_backward_step`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/controlled_markov.py`; canonical namespace `FEP071`.

Invariant: Finite sophisticated expected-free-energy recursion reoptimizes after each observation-dependent update, and a two-stage witness separates feedback from every fixed open-loop second action. Separately, under the explicit BayesianBeliefModel filter-soundness and full-support premises, the generative-model specialization agrees with the reachable-belief POMDP optimum; this stronger statement does not make the generic primary Bayesian.

Assumptions: Belief, action, and observation carriers are finite and Action is nonempty so each finite argmin exists. The theorem is a maintained finite-horizon recursion with an exact Boolean two-stage witness; it does not prove continuous-state or infinite-horizon sophisticated planning. SophisticatedEFEModel supplies a real stage objective and an observation-indexed update. This generic recursion does not certify Bayes correctness or derive the stage objective from mutual information. Reviewed 2026-10-23 in docs/relations-endpoint-adjudication.md (FORM-S11): FEP.PerceptionActionLoop.bayesSophisticatedValue_eq_pomdpOptimum and bayesRiskAmbiguity_eq_sophisticated provide the separate Bayesian specialization on a finite filter-closed indexed belief family with positive-evidence update soundness, nonempty actions, and full support at every indexed belief. boolBayes_rederives_feedbackWitness re-derives the ordinary Boolean mismatch-cost tree through the filter; it is not a full-support EFE witness. boolKeepPrior_not_bayesian and boolKeepPrior_agreement_fails expose the observation-ignoring update boundary. No null-observation Bayes identity, full-simplex enumeration, continuous-state or infinite-horizon result is claimed.

Non-vacuity: In the witness, false and true observations choose different continuation actions, and the resulting expected cost is strictly below that of either fixed Boolean continuation.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep071_sophisticatedEFE_backward_step, fep071_twoStage_feedback_changes_action, and fep071_twoStage_feedback_strictly_better.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep071_sophisticatedEFE_backward_step
    (model : SophisticatedEFEModel Belief Action Observation)
    (horizon : ℕ) (belief : Belief) :
    sophisticatedEFEValue model (horizon + 1) belief =
      model.stageEFE horizon belief (sophisticatedEFEAction model horizon belief) +
        ∑ observation,
          model.observationLaw belief (sophisticatedEFEAction model horizon belief)
              observation *
            sophisticatedEFEValue model horizon
              (model.update belief (sophisticatedEFEAction model horizon belief)
                observation)
```

### fep-072: Hidden-Markov Forward Filtering Recursion

Family: `temporal-and-hierarchical-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep072.FEP072.fep072_forward_filtering_recursion`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/temporal_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP072`.

Invariant: One positive-evidence finite hidden-Markov filtering step reconstructs its predicted state-observation joint, with an exact asymmetric Boolean posterior witness.

Assumptions: State and observation carriers are finite, transition and emission rows are normalized, and observed evidence is strictly positive. No filter is claimed at zero evidence and no continuous-state or Kalman–Bucy limit is inferred.

Non-vacuity: The asymmetric Boolean HMM assigns true posterior mass 20/23, while the zero-evidence theorem explicitly excludes an undefined normalization.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep072_forward_filtering_recursion, fep072_bool_forward_mass, and fep072_zero_evidence_boundary.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep072_forward_filtering_recursion (prior : FiniteLaw State)
    (transition : FiniteKernel State State)
    (emission : FiniteKernel State Observation) (observation : Observation)
    (hEvidence : 0 < forwardEvidence prior transition emission observation)
    (state : State) :
    forwardFilter prior transition emission observation hEvidence state *
        forwardEvidence prior transition emission observation =
      forwardPrediction prior transition state * emission state observation
```

### fep-073: Backward Information-Message Recursion

Family: `temporal-and-hierarchical-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep073.FEP073.fep073_backward_message_recursion`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/temporal_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP073`.

Invariant: Finite backward information messages obey the exact future-observation recursion and preserve nonnegativity from one message to the preceding one.

Assumptions: State and observation carriers are finite and transition and emission rows are normalized. Nonnegativity requires the supplied next message to be pointwise nonnegative; the theorem does not normalize messages or assert a limiting smoother.

Non-vacuity: An identity transition with differing likelihoods of the observed symbol gives a state-dependent backward message. Identical transition rows may erase that dependence even when emission rows differ.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep073_backward_message_recursion and fep073_backward_message_nonnegative for arbitrary finite future lists.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep073_backward_message_recursion
    (transition : FiniteKernel State State)
    (emission : FiniteKernel State Observation) (observation : Observation)
    (future : List Observation) (state : State) :
    backwardMessage transition emission (observation :: future) state =
      backwardMessageStep transition emission observation
        (backwardMessage transition emission future) state
```

### fep-074: Forward–Backward Smoothing Factorization

Family: `temporal-and-hierarchical-inference`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep074.FEP074.fep074_forwardBackward_smoothing_factorization`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/temporal_inference.py`; canonical namespace `FEP074`.

Invariant: At positive normalizer, a finite smoothing marginal factors into forward law times backward information, and one-step forward and backward computations agree on observation evidence.

Assumptions: The forward law is normalized, the backward message is nonnegative, and its weighted normalizer is strictly positive. The statement covers a finite factorization and does not assert positivity when all backward weights vanish.

Non-vacuity: Backward weights differing at two positive-forward-mass states reweight their odds; differences confined to zero-forward-mass states do not affect the normalized law.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep074_forwardBackward_smoothing_factorization and fep074_forward_backward_evidence_agreement.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep074_forwardBackward_smoothing_factorization
    (filtered : FiniteLaw State) (backward : State → ℝ)
    (hBackward : ∀ state, 0 ≤ backward state)
    (hNormalizer : 0 < smoothingNormalizer filtered backward) (state : State) :
    forwardBackwardSmoothing filtered backward hBackward hNormalizer state *
        smoothingNormalizer filtered backward =
      filtered state * backward state
```

Assumptions: Upper, middle, and observation carriers are finite and both conditional maps are FiniteKernel values. The result establishes two-level factorization only, not identifiability of latent levels or an unbounded hierarchy.

Non-vacuity: Nonidentity upper and lower kernels with a nondegenerate top law produce a predictive mixture that genuinely sums over the intermediate level.

### fep-079: Blanket Factorization and Conditional-Mutual-Information Equivalence

Family: `causal-blankets-and-interventions`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep079.FEP079.fep079_blanketFactorization_iff_conditionalMutualInformation_zero`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/causal_blankets_interventions.py`; canonical namespace `FEP079`.

Invariant: On a finite conditional-blanket carrier with full blanket support, vanishing conditional mutual information is equivalent to pointwise internal–external factorization.

Assumptions: Blanket, internal, and external carriers are finite and every blanket atom is strictly positive for the equivalence. The static-model specialization proves zero conditional mutual information even with null blanket atoms, but the theorem does not identify a general physical Markov blanket.

Non-vacuity: Static finite blanket models instantiate exact conditional product rows, and the full-support premise ensures that no nonfactorizing row is hidden behind a zero blanket probability.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep079_blanketFactorization_iff_conditionalMutualInformation_zero and fep079_staticBlanket_conditionalMutualInformation_zero.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. theorem fep079_blanketFactorization_iff_conditionalMutualInformation_zero
```lean
    (model : ConditionalBlanketModel Blanket Internal External)

    conditionalMutualInformation model = 0 ↔ Factorizes model
```

### fep-080: Shared-Conditional Mixture Preservation

Family: `causal-blankets-and-interventions`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep080.FEP080.fep080_sharedConditional_mixture_preservation`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/causal_blankets_interventions.py`; canonical namespace `FEP080`.

Invariant: Convex mixing of two finite input laws commutes exactly with forming their joint laws when both components share the same conditional kernel.

Assumptions: Input and output carriers are finite, the mixing weight lies in the closed unit interval, and both components use one identical normalized kernel. The result does not cover mixtures whose conditional mechanisms differ.

Non-vacuity: The half-mixture witness assigns mass one half to each Boolean atom, and distinct component laws may still produce a genuinely mixed joint after the shared kernel is applied.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep080_sharedConditional_mixture_preservation and fep080_boolHalfMixture_nonvacuity with both convex-weight bounds.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep080_sharedConditional_mixture_preservation
    (weight : ℝ) (hWeightNonneg : 0 ≤ weight)
    (hWeightLeOne : weight ≤ 1)
    (left right : FiniteLaw Input) (kernel : FiniteKernel Input Output) :
    kernel.joint (mixLaw weight hWeightNonneg hWeightLeOne left right) =
      mixLaw weight hWeightNonneg hWeightLeOne
        (kernel.joint left) (kernel.joint right)
```

Family: `causal-blankets-and-interventions`. Disposition: `structural_proxy`.

Invariant: Two conditionally factorized subsystems retain zero paired-row mutual information under an arbitrarily coupled joint blanket law.

Assumptions: Every carrier is finite and subsystem internal and external kernels factor by their own blanket coordinates. The conclusion is rowwise conditional mutual information for paired variables; it does not assert that the joint blanket factorizes or that the complete subsystems are marginally independent.

Non-vacuity: A diagonal Boolean blanket law has zero cross mass and strictly positive blanket mutual information, demonstrating genuine coupling despite exact within-row conditional factorization.

### fep-082: Finite Intervention-Kernel Normalization

Family: `causal-blankets-and-interventions`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep082.FEP082.fep082_interventionKernel_normalization`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/causal_blankets_interventions.py`; canonical namespace `FEP082`.

Invariant: A hard finite intervention kernel places unit mass on the chosen value, zero mass on every distinct value, and remains normalized in every context.

Assumptions: Context and value carriers are finite and value equality is decidable. The kernel represents a deterministic do-assignment only; it does not by itself encode graph surgery, confounding assumptions, or interventional identifiability.

Non-vacuity: On any value carrier with two distinct elements, one receives mass one and the other mass zero independently of context, exercising both sides of the hard intervention.

### fep-084: Ordered Finite Causal Factorization

Family: `causal-blankets-and-interventions`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep084.FEP084.fep084_orderedFiniteCausal_factorization`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/causal_blankets_interventions.py`; canonical namespace `FEP084`.

Invariant: The maintained four-node observational law is exactly its ordered product of two root laws and two parent-conditioned kernels, and that product normalizes.

Assumptions: Root, non-descendant, mediator, and outcome carriers are finite and every component is a normalized finite law or kernel. The carrier records one fixed parent order rather than a general acyclic-graph factorization calculus.

Non-vacuity: Nondegenerate root laws and nonconstant mediator and outcome kernels yield a dependent joint whose normalization requires summing every ordered factor.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep084_orderedFiniteCausal_factorization and fep084_orderedFiniteCausal_normalization on the four-node carrier.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep084_orderedFiniteCausal_factorization
    (model : OrderedFourNodeModel Root NonDescendant Mediator Outcome)
    (root : Root) (nonDescendant : NonDescendant)
    (mediator : Mediator) (outcome : Outcome) :
    orderedJoint model (((root, nonDescendant), mediator), outcome) =
      ((model.rootLaw root * model.nonDescendantLaw nonDescendant) *
          model.mediatorGivenRoot root mediator) *
        model.outcomeGivenParents (nonDescendant, mediator) outcome
```

### fep-085: Local Markov Property from Ordered Factorization

Family: `causal-blankets-and-interventions`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep085.FEP085.fep085_localMarkov_factorization_from_ordered`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/causal_blankets_interventions.py`; canonical namespace `FEP085`.

Invariant: In the ordered four-node carrier, conditioning on the outcome's two parents factorizes the outcome from its non-parent predecessor root and forces their finite mutual information to zero.

Assumptions: All carriers are finite and mediator evidence must be strictly positive. The authored local product row is defined even at a null non-descendant value; interpreting it as an identified conditional on the joint parent event additionally requires positive non-descendant mass. Existing reconstruction recovers the ordered joint atom after multiplying by both parent-evidence factors. This is one maintained ordering, not general d-separation, faithfulness or native measurable conditional independence.

Non-vacuity: The Boolean model conditions on a mediator event of positive half mass and obtains zero mutual information, while a separate theorem marks zero mediator evidence as outside the conditional construction.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep085_localMarkov_factorization_from_ordered, fep085_localMarkov_mutualInformation_zero, fep085_bool_localMarkov_nonvacuity, and fep085_zeroEvidence_boundary.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep085_localMarkov_factorization_from_ordered
    (model : OrderedFourNodeModel Root NonDescendant Mediator Outcome)
    (nonDescendant : NonDescendant) (mediator : Mediator)
    (hEvidence : 0 < mediatorEvidence model mediator) :
    localMarkovConditional model nonDescendant mediator hEvidence =
      (model.mediatorGivenRoot.posterior model.rootLaw mediator hEvidence).product
        (model.outcomeGivenParents.row (nonDescendant, mediator))
```

### fep-086: Precision-Weighted Prediction-Error Energy

Family: `predictive-coding-and-generalized-coordinates`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep086.FEP086.fep086_precisionWeighted_zero_iff_exactPrediction`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/predictive_coding_generalized.py`; canonical namespace `FEP086`.

Invariant: Nonnegative precision yields nonnegative scalar prediction-error energy, and under positive precision zero energy occurs exactly at exact prediction.

Assumptions: The carrier is scalar real arithmetic. Nonnegativity needs precision at least zero and separation needs precision strictly positive; zero precision cannot distinguish any estimate from the observation.

Non-vacuity: Observation one, estimate zero, and precision two have exact unit energy, so the energy and its separating zero condition are not vacuous.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep086_precisionWeighted_predictionError_nonnegative, fep086_precisionWeighted_zero_iff_exactPrediction, and fep086_unitError_nonvacuity.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep086_precisionWeighted_zero_iff_exactPrediction
    {precision : ℝ} (hPrecision : 0 < precision)
    (observation estimate : ℝ) :
    precisionEnergy precision observation estimate = 0 ↔
      estimate = observation
```

### fep-087: Hierarchical Predictive-Coding Energy Decomposition

Family: `predictive-coding-and-generalized-coordinates`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep087.FEP087.fep087_hierarchicalPredictiveCoding_decomposition`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/predictive_coding_generalized.py`; canonical namespace `FEP087`.

Invariant: Finite hierarchical predictive-coding energy decomposes into the zeroth precision-weighted error plus all successor-level errors and is nonnegative when every precision is nonnegative.

Assumptions: The hierarchy is finite and energies are explicit scalar quadratics. Nonnegativity requires pointwise nonnegative precision; the result does not model neural implementation, learned precision, or an infinite hierarchy.

Non-vacuity: A concrete two-level hierarchy has both levels contribute and evaluates to eleven, ruling out a decomposition that silently drops the upper level.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep087_hierarchicalPredictiveCoding_decomposition, fep087_hierarchicalPredictiveCoding_nonnegative, and fep087_twoLevel_decomposition_nonvacuity.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep087_hierarchicalPredictiveCoding_decomposition {depth : ℕ}
    (precision error : Fin (depth + 1) → ℝ) :
    hierarchicalEnergy precision error =
      precision 0 / 2 * error 0 ^ 2 +
        ∑ level : Fin depth,
          precision level.succ / 2 * error level.succ ^ 2
```

### fep-090: Finite-Jet Generalized-Filtering Correction Equation

Family: `predictive-coding-and-generalized-coordinates`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep090.FEP090.fep090_finiteJet_generalizedFiltering_correctionEquation`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/predictive_coding_generalized.py`; canonical namespace `FEP090`.

Invariant: One finite-jet generalized-filtering Euler step equals the current coordinate plus a truncated shift minus the certified quadratic energy gradient.

Assumptions: Step size and coordinate precisions are real and the jet order is finite. The pointwise correction has an exact scalar derivative witness, but the theorem does not identify an infinite generalized flow or prove stability of repeated coupled-jet updates.

Non-vacuity: A concrete first-order half-step gives exact nonzero corrections 3/2 and 1/2 at the two retained coordinates, while the top-coordinate theorem removes the truncated shift term.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep090_finiteJet_generalizedFiltering_correctionEquation, fep090_finiteJet_coordinateGradient_hasDerivAt, fep090_finiteJet_truncation_visible, and fep090_firstOrder_correction_nonvacuity.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep090_finiteJet_generalizedFiltering_correctionEquation
    {order : ℕ} (stepSize : ℝ) (precision : ℕ → ℝ)
    (target estimate : FiniteJet order) (degree : ℕ) :
    (generalizedFilteringStep stepSize precision target estimate).coefficient degree =
      estimate.coefficient degree + stepSize *
        ((shift 1 estimate).coefficient degree -
          precisionEnergyGradient (precision degree)
            (target.coefficient degree) (estimate.coefficient degree))
```

### fep-091: Precision Modulation of Prediction Error

Family: `predictive-coding-and-generalized-coordinates`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep091.FEP091.fep091_precisionModulation_energy_mono`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/predictive_coding_generalized.py`; canonical namespace `FEP091`.

Invariant: Increasing nonnegative precision cannot decrease a fixed scalar prediction-error energy, and scaling precision scales its exact gradient by the same factor.

Assumptions: Energy monotonicity assumes a nonnegative lower precision and an ordered upper precision. Gradient scaling is algebraic for arbitrary real scale, including negative values, but only nonnegative precision retains an energy weighting interpretation.

Non-vacuity: Doubling precision from one to two at a unit prediction error exactly doubles the energy and makes the inequality strict.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep091_precisionModulation_energy_mono, fep091_precisionModulation_gradient_scale, and fep091_doubledPrecision_nonvacuity.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep091_precisionModulation_energy_mono
    {lower upper : ℝ} (hLowerNonneg : 0 ≤ lower)
    (hPrecision : lower ≤ upper)
    (observation estimate : ℝ) :
    0 ≤ precisionEnergy lower observation estimate ∧
      precisionEnergy lower observation estimate ≤
        precisionEnergy upper observation estimate
```

### fep-092: Quadratic Predictive-Coding Convergence

Family: `predictive-coding-and-generalized-coordinates`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep092.FEP092.fep092_quadraticPredictiveCoding_error_tendsto_zero`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/predictive_coding_generalized.py`; canonical namespace `FEP092`.

Invariant: Exact scalar quadratic prediction updates contract energy by the squared factor one minus step size, strictly decrease positive-precision nonzero error for step sizes in the open interval zero to two, and drive error to zero.

Assumptions: Convergence requires 0 < stepSize < 2 for the authored residual update; strict descent also needs positive precision and unequal target and estimate. A raw negative-gradient step with learning rate alpha has effective stepSize alpha*precision. The theorem is scalar exact-quadratic and does not prove stability of coupled finite-jet or nonlinear predictive-coding updates.

Non-vacuity: At precision two and half-step size, a unit error has energy one, moves to estimate one half, and drops to energy one quarter.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep092_quadraticPredictiveCoding_contraction, fep092_quadraticPredictiveCoding_strictDecrease, fep092_quadraticPredictiveCoding_error_tendsto_zero, and fep092_halfStep_decrease_nonvacuity.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose. This is neither a certified translation, Lean elaboration, a minimal kernel dependency graph, nor LLM execution. A proposition-looking binder is not classified as a proved hypothesis by this parser.

Exact primary Lean statement:

```lean
theorem fep092_quadraticPredictiveCoding_error_tendsto_zero
    {stepSize : ℝ} (hStepPositive : 0 < stepSize)
    (hStepBelowTwo : stepSize < 2) (target estimate : ℝ) :
    Tendsto
      (fun iterations => predictionError target
        (iteratePredictionUpdate stepSize target iterations estimate))
      atTop (nhds 0)
```

### fep-093: Forward and Reverse Finite Path-Law Ratio

Family: `path-space-stochastic-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `formalized`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep093.FEP093.fep093_forward_reverse_pathLaw_ratio`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/path_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP093`.

Invariant: A finite forward path law is reconstructed pointwise from its supported reverse-aligned law and likelihood ratio, while both path laws normalize and path reversal is involutive.

Assumptions: The path carrier is finite and reverse-aligned mass must be strictly positive for the ratio reconstruction. FinitePathProtocol stores normalized laws and an involution; it does not derive reversal or reverse dynamics from a microscopic process.

Non-vacuity: Unequal full-support forward and reverse-aligned laws yield nonunit path ratios, while an involution with a two-path swap exercises a genuine reversal.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep093_forward_reverse_pathLaw_ratio, fep093_pathLaws_normalized, and fep093_pathReversal_involutive.

Source-grounded semantic analysis: exact Lean source slices and lexical roles, joined to maintained review prose.
```lean
theorem fep093_forward_reverse_pathLaw_ratio
    (protocol : FinitePathProtocol Path)
    (hReverse : ∀ path, 0 < protocol.reverseAligned path) (path : Path) :
    pathRatio protocol path * protocol.reverseAligned path =
      protocol.forward path
```

### fep-094: Entropy Production as Path KL

Family: `path-space-stochastic-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `structural_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep094.FEP094.fep094_entropyProduction_as_pathKL`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/path_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP094`.

Invariant: Mean finite path entropy production is the KL divergence from the forward to reverse-aligned path law, is nonnegative, and under full support equals the forward expectation of the pointwise log ratio.

Assumptions: The primary equality is the maintained definition of entropyProduction. Expected-log-ratio expansion requires strict support of both path laws, and the project's totalized real KL is not an extended divergence at zero reverse mass.

### fep-095: Detailed Fluctuation Symmetry

Family: `path-space-stochastic-thermodynamics`. Disposition: `conditional_proxy`.

Primary theorem: `fep_fep095.FEP095.fep095_detailedFluctuation_symmetry`.

Body owner: `src/fep_lean/catalogue/bodies/path_thermodynamics.py`; canonical namespace `FEP095`.

Invariant: Under full path support, reverse-aligned mass times exponential pointwise entropy production reconstructs forward mass, and an exchanging reversal makes entropy production antisymmetric.

Assumptions: Forward and reverse-aligned laws are strictly positive. Antisymmetry further assumes that the recorded involution exchanges the two aligned laws in both directions; these exchange laws are not derived from protocol dynamics.

Non-vacuity: A two-path protocol with swapped unequal full-support masses has nonzero pointwise entropy production whose sign reverses on the paired path.

Canonical acceptance probe: native Lean compile of fep095_detailedFluctuation_symmetry and fep095_entropyProduction_reversal_antisymmetry with both exchange hypotheses.

```lean
theorem fep095_detailedFluctuation_symmetry
    (protocol : FinitePathProtocol Path)
    (hForward : ∀ path, 0 < protocol.forward path)
    (hReverse : ∀ path, 0 < protocol.reverseAligned path) (path : Path) :
    protocol.reverseAligned path *
        Real.exp (pathwiseEntropyProduction protocol path) =
      protocol.forward path
```
